## §4 THE ONE PATROL STAGE AND THE ROUND (PR-0)

### 4.1 The stage (per tick; every preset where `patrolsEnabled` is lit) — `src/domain/patrols/patrolStage.js :: advancePatrols`
A LEAF called at the head of `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` right AFTER ST's site stage (which follows MN's menace stage; OD-MN-20, ST §6.1), before calamity's own flag gate; +3 host lines (the call and the merge of `{ worldState, newsEntries, receipts }`); zero kernel lines. Car-0 (a MEASUREMENT before any PR line): re-measure the host with MN's and ST's leaves (461 + theirs + 3 ≤ 800); MN-0's four STOP conditions inherited verbatim; ⛔ STOP at build if the host cannot carry a third leaf or if MN-0/ST-0 chose another host — then PR's stage follows theirs. Each tick, in this fixed order, every step writing only through `patrols/patrolLedger.js`:
1. **Losses that recovered** drop out of the count; losses past the horizon are dropped.
2. **The envelope and the fielded count** for every settlement (§3), codepoint order; and each town's `forkSince` (§2.7) written, kept or deleted by its peace-posture fork's run this week [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W1].
3. **Detachments advance** (§5): converging riders arrive (their leg priced by `namedPersonLegTicks`), engaged detachments resolve their hearing's verdict or default (§11.2), a hunt moves to this week's predicted place (§5.5) and, where its quarry fled by a way the town does not know whole, rolls its GLEANING (§5.5; BD's write) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — L-BD 11e], finished errands DISPERSE (a receipt) — a hunt also when its trail goes cold — and detachments past the rest bound send their riders `resting` (§7.3).
4. **New detachments form** from believed threats (§5.2), hunts (§5.5: a pursued body believed in the land; a mission taken under a named leader), looks (§9.2), escorts (§10.4) and the DM's order — each drawing riders from the round in reverse priority (the least important stretch loses its rider first).
5. **The round, the net and the placement** — the remaining riders are placed on their halves by the round's order, the rider-time allocation and the periodic clock (§2.3: one place per rider) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the carry]; then, in a kingdom whose crown's own hold reads strong, THE NET (§10.3 (d)) places the crown's riders and each member's lent riders (up to its `lend(m)`, §10.3 (c)) on the halves the homes left unwatched where a need stands — after every home's own round, so a home's first call is never displaced [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7]; the stage writes the coming week's `placement` (`next` for t+1; the old `next` moved to `lived`; the coverage ring advanced) — the ONE placement every consumer of t+1 reads (PR4) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-15]; and from the `lived` rows of the week just lived it writes the two facts other owners keep or read: each member's SEASON TALLY `weeksCovered` (§2.7, B.9) and, through THE BODIES' one writer `bodies/hiddenWays.js`, the `watched` mark of every stored known way whose junction half its riders stood on (BD §2.3; the mark dropped by BD's writer after a season with none) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.9 · PR-X2 · BD's round-5 route (d)].
6. **Reports** — every patrol that SAW something this week (a believed threat confirmed, a crossing witnessed, a neighbour's riders seen on the neighbour's half at a shared gate, a look's finding, a catch, a clash) emits a `patrol_report` (§9.3) — the six `finding` words, `riders_seen` (§8.3's first producer) among them [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V11: the trigger list omitted `riders_seen`, the planners' own producer].

**Clock** (requirement 9): weekly. The stage runs at `advanceCalamity`'s head (kernel l.2527) — AFTER every consumer of the week: `ageRoamingStressors` (l.510, the counter term's stamp), `advanceSettlementSupply` (l.843, banditry and smuggling), `advanceRumorLedgers` (l.2097, reports), `advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse` (l.2194, THE ONE PERSON STAGE's encounters), `advanceEmbattlement` (l.2391, the slow half), `advanceArmyTransit` (l.2442, the witness and the screen). So the placement it writes at tick t for t+1 is exactly the placement every consumer reads in t+1 — read-last, write-next made STRUCTURAL by the stored digest, never by re-derivation; a pin asserts that every consumer of one week names the same placement (R30). THE BODIES' stage runs AFTER this one at the same head (BD §2.7: "Bodies move AFTER the riders are placed") and reads the row whose `forTick` is ITS tick — after step 5 that row is `lived` — so a body meets the week's true watch and R30 holds for it too; PL's protection and friction rows read the SEASON TALLY the stage counts from those same `lived` rows (`bannerCoverOf`, §10.3 (f)) — PL's polity stage runs later in the same tick (`advanceTreatiesWithDisposition`, kernel l.2752, after `advanceCalamity` l.2527 — PL's measured order, `cure/D-PL-r5.md` §1 (d)), so at a season's LAST tick the tally already holds the whole season [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes · A.7: two more consumers of the one placement, named] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.9: the one-week `lived` read replaced by the season tally]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-15: nothing was stored, so "read-last/write-next" had no write to read and consumers re-derived from different mid-tick states (ordering CONFIRMED, `cure/D-PR-r2.md` §1(f)).]

### 4.2 Why weekly placement and not a stepper
A stepper would put patrols in WC's counts-mover manifest and in the frozen transit kernel's collision pass, and would make the map draw marching order the table never sees. A weekly placement is exactly the host document's own model (presence per stretch per week, by address — a phase fixed by hash), it costs one stored row per town per week, and it gives the DM the only fact that matters: which roads were watched this week. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — macro over micro (the owner, 06:32).]

---

## §5 REACT · GROUP · DISPERSE (the owner's first word: "react and have reasonable routes, be able to group and disperse depending on belief and effect")

### 5.1 What the town believes is out there (THE ONE THREAT READ)
`believedThreatsIn(worldState, snapshot, settlementId, tick) → [{ ref, place, band, sinceTick, source }]` — the town's belief, never the truth (K3), from the landed carriers each threat already has:
- a MENACE — MN's court picture "derived on read from the rumor records it holds (last seen where and when; the tale's might through a might↔magnitude map)" (MN §4.5); its band = the believed might;
- a DEN — a site attached to the town held by an UNTIED holder (ST §2.1: a body — a gang or a beast, THE BODIES' ref `{ kind: 'body', … }` — a menace, or a villain; never a conclave by holder kind, whose site is a church's, RULINGS-ROUND4 A.3 — an `outlawed` or `curbed` flock's site is ST's SEIZURE by the court's stance, ST §6.1 step 6c, RULINGS-ROUND5 A.1, the court's own act and force, never the riders' choice) AS THE COURT BELIEVES IT — ST's one read `believedDenOf(worldState, courtId, siteId, snapshot)`, which reads the holder's own belief record and never ST's (RULINGS-ROUND5 A.4 · A.5: "ST's record keeps only the TRUTH of the holder"), so a den that LEFT unseen is still believed there until word comes [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.4 · A.5 · A.1, PR's echo: the den's belief named at its one read]: not under the court veil (`keptSecret`, ST §2.4 — the map's `hidden` word was withdrawn there), for a kept-secret den is learned only by WITNESS — a look's finding (§9.2 (b)) or the warden's ways (§5.5) — and makes no `den_unanswered` (RULINGS-ROUND4 A.16) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.3 (the struck `band`/`cult` holder kinds) · A.16 (the veil's word and its witness)];
- a BODY — THE BODIES' gang, conclave or beast the town has heard of: LISTED by BD's `believedBodies(worldState, observerId)` → `{ bodyId, bodyKind, lastWhereId, lastWhat, lastTick }` and READ, each, through the town's ONE belief record of it, `believedBodyOf(worldState, townId, bodyId)` → `{ known, mightBand?, baseId?, lastWhereId?, lastWhat?, lastTick?, leaderRef?, source }` (BD §2.4: from the town's rumor records carrying the body's id and the sightings registered as rows of the same source — a site's sighting, a patrol's report, an INFORMER's word where the commons fear the body, a caught operative's confession; truth in the omniscient arm) — its band the believed `mightBand`, its last place and tick the record's [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD→PR route (9): `believedBodies` carries no might, so "its band the believed might" had no source; the informer rows reach the riders through the one record]; a THREAT the riders ride against only where the court's stance toward it is `hunted` (a gang) or `outlawed` (a conclave), or it is a beast (§5.5; BD §7.1) — a body the court lets be is no threat to ride at; WC-12's located brigand body IS BD's gang record (BD's route to WC) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (6): the slot "WC-12's located unit (UNBUILT: a slot; until it lands the ambient raider stressor has no body to ride to)" is filled];
- a HOSTILE ARMY near — an army the town has witnessed in its land or on a stretch of its round (PS's crossing rows with `witnessed`; the war's own front reads);
- a CROSSING UNIT — a PS crossing in the town's land in state `trespassing` or `overstayed`;
- a REPORT — any `patrol_report` or rumor telling the town holds placing such a thing on its land, true or FALSE (IN's plant road — the `plant_taken` / `lure_sprung` kinds IN registers (IN-2), a lie that "needs a liar" with a traceable source (PT §11); a provocateur's staged report rides the same road (CH)).
Under the omniscient arm (no rumor road) the read returns the truth inside the town's territory (every belief read does); false reports exist only where the rumor road runs (infoMode `full`) — the TIER line says so.

### 5.2 Converge and group
For each believed threat, in descending believed band (codepoint tie-break): if one patrol's band ≥ the threat's believed band, ONE rider leaves the round (`converging`, then `engaged`); else the town forms a DETACHMENT of the fewest riders whose summed latent strength reaches the threat's band (DRAFT: latent points per band — `thin` 15 · `modest` 30 · `capable` 50 · `strong` 70 · `formidable` 85 — summed and re-banded), drawing from the round in reverse priority, and never more than `fielded − 1` (one rider always stays on the round's first half) — EXCEPT a ONE-WEEK act (a look; a response to a threat within one hop of the seat), which may take the town's last rider at the round's cost, so a hamlet's one rider can look and a village's two can ride together against what is at its door [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-26: below a town the third and fourth words were inert]. If the whole fielded count cannot reach the threat's band, the riders DO NOT GROUP TO FIGHT: one rider watches and reports, and the town's believed threat stands for its court's own deciders (the chooser, relief, the menace's response — §6's consumers read it). Arrival takes `namedPersonLegTicks({ nominalWeeks: hopWeeks(digest, seat, place, season) })` weeks, `UNPRICED_LEG_WEEKS` per graph hop aspatially.

### 5.3 What a detachment does when it arrives (one contest each, never PR's own law)
| The threat | The act | The contest (its owner) |
|---|---|---|
| a den in a site | `clear` | ST's `site_cleared` (CLIMAX; "the force's strength (its `offensive`, or the patrol's band) against the holder's might on `SIEGE_VERDICT_BANDS`, one keyed draw") — ST §6.1 step 6 already names "a grouped patrol" as a clearer |
| a menace | report and withdraw, unless the detachment's band ≥ the menace's might — then `clear` | MN's `menace_met` (CLIMAX; options `engage · withdraw`, then `menace_beaten · army_beaten · indecisive`) with the detachment as the force — a PRODUCER ROW in MN's commit (MN §7's trigger list gains "a patrol detachment's arrival"); MN's `army_beaten` reads "an army or a detachment" — one vocabulary, MN's bracket (RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 6) |
| a BODY its court pursues — a `hunted` gang, an `outlawed` conclave, a beast (THE BODIES; WC-12's located brigands are BD's gangs) — on the road, a trail the town knows, or in the country | `pursue` (THE HUNT, §5.5) | `patrol_engages` (§11.2) — PR's band contest on `SIEGE_VERDICT_BANDS` with BD's `bodyStrength` as the foe's force, one keyed draw, PR's one vocabulary (BD §9.3: "PR's `patrol_engages` — `driven_off · screened · withdrew · cut_down`, PR's band contest, PR's card") [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (6) · A.3: the slot is filled; the r3 "WC-12's contest and its vocabulary (`suppression · worn_down · absorbed · buy_off`) … whichever lands second" is WITHDRAWN — those words close a brigand SIEGE (WC-12's `EMBATTLED_RESOLUTIONS`, `cure/L-BD.md` §1(a)), never a road fight] |
| an enemy army marching on the town, or crossing its land toward another | `screen` | `patrol_engages` (§9.1) — PR hosts the screen's card in either case; PS keeps the home-defence battle (RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 1) |
| a unit crossing the town's land (PS) | witness and escort only (PS's ladder answers it) | PS's cards; a `barred` answer's contest is PS's (§10.4) |
| nothing (the threat was never there) | look, find nothing, disperse | none — a `patrol_report` "found nothing" (§9.3) |

### 5.4 Disperse
A detachment DISPERSES — its riders return to `on_round` the next week, a `patrol_dispersed` receipt with `causedBy` the finishing verdict or report — when its contest resolves, when the threat is no longer believed (the town's belief has moved: a later report, the rumor's TTL, the threat gone — a hunt's COLD TRAIL is one, §5.5), when the DM's order ends it, or at the rest bound (§7.3).

### 5.5 THE HUNT — riders after a body (the owner's ~19:0x word: "patrols when not in routine patrolling are trying to predict and catch up to gangs, beasts, and hostile conclaves"; THE BODIES §9.3, which cites this section; L-BD items 20 · 39 · 40 · 41) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (3) and (5) · L-BD 20 · 39 · 40 · 41: "the hunt is PR's text; BD cites"]
- **WHAT IS HUNTED.** A town's riders pursue a body ONLY where the town's court stance toward it (BD's `courtStanceOf`, BD §7.1) is `hunted` (a gang) or `outlawed` (a conclave), or it is a beast (every court hunts beasts, BD10). A `permitted` or `endowed` conclave and a `tolerated`, `employed` or `harboured` gang are NEVER pursued (the owner: a good conclave in a good town is "not … pursued by patrols"); a `curbed` conclave is WATCHED, never hunted — met and noticed, it is reported (§8.1), not engaged. The stance is the court's, decided in its own hearing (BD's `body_stance`); the riders never choose it. A GANG's default is `hunted` — `tolerated` only where the town's effective tolerance reads ≥ 0.3 or its underworld rules, a patron-less town taking its law from its government (BD §7.1 as cured in round 5: "OTHERWISE → `hunted` (the owner, ~18:3x: "These are public enemies to most")") — so the riders pursue most gangs, and a deity-free world's towns are no safe road [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD-r4's ROUTES to PR ("PR's seek weights make `tolerated` gangs unpursued, which BD-r4-5's default makes the majority"): BD's default is cured, PR's quarry rule reads it unchanged].
- **THE PREDICTION, ON BELIEF.** A pursued body the town believes in or beside its land (§5.1) is ridden for at its PREDICTED place, `predictedPlaceOf(worldState, snapshot, settlementId, bodyId, tick)` (§2.6), from three things the town holds and nothing it does not: (i) its newest sighting — where and what, from its ONE belief record of the body (`believedBodyOf`'s `lastWhereId`, `lastWhat`, `lastTick`; an INFORMER's word where the town's commons fear the body is one of its rows, BD §7.6: "the court's belief sharpens and the hunt's prediction improves (PR's `predictedPlaceOf` reads the sighting)") [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD→PR route (9)]; (ii) the ways it KNOWS (`knownTrailsOf`, THE BODIES' one union of the learned set and the office's ways, B.13) — an unknown way is never predicted; (iii) the body's HABITS, BD's closed `BODY_HABITS` read (its leaf `bodies/bodyHabits.js`, BD-5; BD §9.3: "a gang returns to its den, a pack follows the season, a flock goes where the faith is fractured") — THIS body's own habits where HB carries the body grain (L-BD 39 (c): "the patrol's PREDICTION … reads THIS body's habits (not the kind's)"), else its kind's. Never the body's true position (K3); `null` (nothing to go on) ⇒ no hunt, and the town waits for word.
- **THE PURSUIT.** A detachment with aim `pursue` and `pursuedRef: { kind: 'body', id }` (§2.5) forms by §5.2's rule — one rider whose band reaches the body's believed might, else the fewest riders whose summed latent points reach it, drawn from the round in reverse priority — and each week rides to the predicted place on the light-unit physics (`namedPersonLegTicks`; one place a week, PR1). The CATCH is the ONE encounter law on TRUTH (§8.1's body arm): MET only if the body is really there and the riders stand on that road half, that known trail's junction, that site or that country; NOTICED by the law's draw against the body's cover. Met and noticed ⇒ `patrol_engages` (§11.2), the house's band contest with BD's `bodyStrength`. A catch on a way TEACHES the town the way (`by_catch`, BD §2.3's writer).
- **THE WAYS, FOR RIDERS — one seam, one price (B.4).** A detachment rides a hidden way only as master's hidden franchise allows, and that franchise "fails CLOSED" (`routeNetworkConsumers.js :: mayUseHiddenPaths`: "a lane that forgets to declare itself gets the army's answer"): PR DECLARES ONE token, `patrol`, on `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS` at PR-2's unit (a declared change on a landed access law, the form B.4 gives THE BODIES' `body`; the owner's 18:33 word — "a warden … has access to hidden routes network as part of their job" — and 22:19 — "knowledge of a hidden path can be gleaned from a regular body if in pursuit of an enemy"), and PR's mover passes it ONLY for a `pursue` detachment within the way's capacity (`modest`, BD §5.2: "a body of thirty, never a column") on a way its town KNOWS — the office's ways whole, a learned way as far as its `upTo` — every other patrol move passing no hidden kind (the round, a screen, an escort, a look and every larger detachment take the road: "armies may not" stands for them); a way ridden is priced at the landed `gradeLegMultiplier('hidden')` 2 (= `HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN` 2, "PINNED" equal) on the one leg physics, never a factor of PR's; a patrol NEVER CROSSES a border by a way (PS §5.5: "a patrol never holds a transit clock, and it never takes a hidden way" — its crossing beyond its land is the stretch's, through PS's reads); the way's rung is the route ledger's and PR's riding adds no use of its own (THE BODIES' use counter is the bodies') [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.4 ("PR … RE-POINT[S] [its] trail seams to the landed rung — one seam, one price") · RULINGS-ROUND4 §E's landed price: the r4 "a WARDEN-LED hunt may ride the trails" named no access law and no price; PLAUSIBLE until PR-2's car-0 measures that PR's leg leaf can carry the hidden kind (its legs are `namedPersonLegTicks` over `hopWeeks`, H3's `planWanderLeg` the landed precedent with "`gradeMultiplier: hop.hidden ? NPC_CONSEQUENCES_TUNING.HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN : 1,`")].
- **GLEANING IN PURSUIT (L-BD 11e; BD §2.3: "THE PATROLS' gleaning roll, PR's text, BD's write").** A `pursue` detachment whose quarry FLED BY A WAY its town does not know whole — the body truly left the place the riders reach by a way (truth: the sign is on the ground, as the encounter is) — rolls ONCE per pursuit week on the stream `bodies:glean:<unitKey>:<trailKey>:<tick>` (BD's stream name; `unitKey = 'patrol:' + detachmentId`, PS §2.1's key) to learn the way NODE BY NODE: `p = clamp(GLEAN_BASE × sign × ground × season × craft × home × length × told × habit, 0, GLEAN_CAP)` — SIGN, the quarry's might band and HASTE (a fleeing body leaves more); GROUND, the way's cover (`cover01`: forest and wetland hide, mountain passes show); SEASON (snow shows tracks, high summer hides them — the one seasonal read); CRAFT, a leader of THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE reads sign a levy cannot (the office's person, below); HOME, the riders on their own country (their own `lived` placement; a kingdom's riders on a member's land count as home only under a strong hold, §10.3 (d)); LENGTH, the weeks the pursuit has run; TOLD, a captive's or an informer's word (BD §7.6); HABIT, the riders' own lessons on HB's curve where it lights — every factor DRAFT, UNMEASURED (`GLEAN_BASE` 0.3, `GLEAN_CAP` 0.8); a success writes `by_pursuit` through BD's one writer `bodies/hiddenWays.js` with the PREFIX reached (`upTo`, one node further a success — "followed as far as the ford"), the knowledge then fading on HB's curve as BD §2.3 writes it; the receipt names what was learned and why ("Kell's riders learned the drover's way as far as the ford: snow on the ground, a gang in haste"). A town that BOUGHT the way from a broker first has nothing to glean (`by_broker`, L-BD 11g) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD's round-5 route to PR (c) · L-BD 11e (the owner, 22:19), applied in this round so none is left unapplied; the base and cap REFINED, vetoable].
- **THE COLD TRAIL.** When the town's newest sighting is older than `COLD_TRAIL_WEEKS` (DRAFT 3, UNMEASURED) — `tick − lastTick`, read from `believedBodyOf` [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD→PR route (9)], so nothing is stored for the dwell — the trail is COLD: the detachment DISPERSES (§5.4) and the round resumes. A sighting by a shadowing rider, a report, a raid heard of — any fresh word — keeps it warm.
- **THE UNRIDDEN ROUND (the first counterforce).** The hunters are off the round: the halves they walked fall to `never` while they ride (§4.1 step 4 takes them from the bottom of the round), and a body that plans on `believedWatch` (§8.3) and has heard the riders went hunting slips through the gap — the gang's chance, by belief only (BD12).
- **SHADOW AND GROUP (the second).** A rider OUTMATCHED by the body's believed might never engages: it SHADOWS — follows the prediction and reports (`patrol_report` `threat_seen`, §9.3), keeping the trail warm — while the town groups (§5.2) or asks an ally (`patrol_joins`, §10.1 (d)); under a strong banner a fellow member's riders may ride to it by right, still that member's decision on a `patrol_joins` card (the banner spares the crossing, never the choice — §10.3 (d)).
- **OVER-PURSUIT (the third).** A hunt is bounded by the rest bound (§7.3) and the cold trail; beyond the town's border it is PS's ladder (an ally welcomes hot pursuit as urgency, §10.1 (c)) or the banner's right (§10.3 (d)); a hunt that rides into a body stronger than it believed is `cut_down` (§7.2) — the price of a wrong belief.
- **THE HUNT LED (L-BD 40; the owner's word 12).** When a mission to hunt a NAMED gang or flock, or a beast pack (BD §9.11: "a pack's threat read by its BELIEVED HARM in the court's land, not by fame"), is TAKEN — its card is THE BODIES' `hunt_taken` (BD §9.11: "TAKING THE MISSION is BD's card `hunt_taken` … never PR's"; STANDING until its one CLIMAX week, the mission's close, and closed early by the DM's act — RULINGS-ROUND5 B.6; lit with `bodyEffectsEnabled`, B.16) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.6 · B.16 · the card's owner named (BD)] — PR's detachment is its unit (the quarry rule above binds a mission too — a `curbed` conclave is never hunted, BD §7.1; BD §9.11 reads L-BD 40's "`suppressed`/`outlawed`" as `outlawed` alone, PR's route applied there): sized to the body's believed band PLUS ONE ("appropriate to scale"; DRAFT, UNMEASURED), never more than `fielded − 1` (one rider stays on the round's first half), and short of it the hunt shadows and asks (above); its `leaderRef` names the leader the card names — by BD §9.11's order: (1) the town's PUBLIC-SECURITY office — a person of THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE holding it, else the sword-holder by L-BD 23's one read (BD's `SWORD_BY_GOVERNMENT` names the faction, the office the person), else the watch's senior seat — (2) a court's retained paragon (CH), (3) a free paragon answering a bounty (CH; a CORPS answering a bounty hunts with its OWN unit, never PR's riders) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the order cited at its owner, BD §9.11]. PR NEVER MOVES A PERSON'S RECORD (CH-V3's law, "two movers never share a record"): a PARAGON leader moves by its OWN mover — CH's purpose `hunt_body` with the body as its target (CH §4.2: "`hunt_body` (not `hunt`, landed five quoted-literal files and THE BODIES' court↔beast stance)") [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — CH→PR route 1 · SEAMS-r4-10 · BD-r4-17's PR half: "CH's aim `hunt`" named a landed literal and BD's own stance word], reading the ONE prediction `predictedPlaceOf` on the one physics, so leader and riders stand together — and its lean on the riders (`paragonLean(…, 'wildland', 'patrol_rider_quality')`, §10.5) applies only in a week it stands with them; an office-holder who is not a paragon is a REFERENCE on the row — its name on the card, its recorded axes on the hunt's two forks (a cautious leader shadows unless a band ahead and keeps a trail a week longer; a reckless one engages at an equal band and rides past the rest bound — over-pursuit as its price; DRAFT ±1, UNMEASURED; the person-habit curve where HB carries the person grain, L-BD 39) — and its residency never moves (macro over micro). THE LEADER NEVER DIES BY THE ENGINE (no named fate): a `cut_down` verdict writes the riders' `losses` row and leaves the leader untouched; the mission's outcome words are its card's (L-BD 40: the body's and the unit's, never the person's), and a paragon's failure is CH's `paragon_disgraced` hearing. ONE HUNT, ONE DEED (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5: "the hearing's OWNER mints: a LED hunt's success is ONE deed minted by the hunt's own door — CH's `hunt_won` at the mission's close, doer the LEADER (`ledBy`), target the body; PR's `patrol_clash` mints NO deed for a led hunt (its row is the clash's record)"): the riders' clashes inside a led hunt are `patrol_engages` verdicts and nothing more; the mission's close mints `hunt_won` (CH's door) or, a hunt that fails as `escaped`, the body's own deed (RN registers it) — PR mints neither [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.5 · SEAMS-r4-7 (one success, two doors) closed on PR's side]. A hunt's coin is never the treasury's `deployments` upkeep: a detachment is no deployment record (`treasury.js :: computeUpkeep` charges `{ ledger, record }` over "`UPKEEP_LEDGERS = Object.freeze(['deployments'])`" only, "the `deployments` one-army ledger"), so its cost is the round it leaves unridden and the riders' own pay (§3.1's PAY gate; the W-COIN `patrols` upkeep row when it lights, §7.1) — a menace-scale COLUMN is a deployment and pays as one (BD §9.11's general) [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — measured at master; BD §9.11's "the TREASURY pays the detachment (the landed DEPLOYMENT upkeep)" is routed to BD].
- **THE WARDEN KNOWS THE WAYS (L-BD 41; the owner's word 13).** Where the town's public-security office is HELD by a person of THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE — for a paragon CH's one predicate `isWardenParagon` (CH §2.2: "`domain === 'wildland'` on the champion side of its LIVING polarity"), for an office-holder who is no paragon its landed roster role `warden` (`generators/factionRoles.js`: "`warden: 'watch'`"), never the display noun — the town's KNOWN ways include every hidden way of the office's JURISDICTION — the home's land, and for a crown's office the kingdom's by the crown's reach (§10.3 (c)) — the OFFICE's knowledge, DERIVED while the office is so held and never stored (a new warden knows on taking the post; a noble or a militia master without the trade knows only what the town has LEARNED, BD §2.3's stored half), and NEVER a warden merely RETAINED by the court who does not hold the office (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.13: "the office knowledge belongs to the person HOLDING the public-security office with the warden archetype (L-BD 41), never to any retained warden (CH's widening struck)"); the union is ONE read, BD's `knownTrailsOf` [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.13 · CH→PR route 2 REFUTED by B.13 (the route asked PR to count "a warden paragon RETAINED by the court"; the chair struck the widening) · BD-r4-23]. On PR's side: (a) a WARDEN-LED hunt may ride the ways — under the riders' franchise above (the office's ways whole, at the landed hidden price), the detachment no larger than the way's capacity (`modest`, BD §5.2: "a body of thirty, never a column"), else it keeps to the road and the junctions [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.4]; (b) the town's riders cover the junctions of every way the office knows (§2.2: the office's ways' junction halves FIRST), and a body that has heard of the warden believes those ways watched (`believedWatch` on a trail key, §8.3) — deterrence by belief: "Kell's warden knows the drover's way; the hill gang has moved its den across the border."
- **CLOCK · TIER · COUNTERFORCE · FELT.** Weekly (the stage, §4.1); `bodiesEnabled` for the quarry, `bodyEffectsEnabled` for a LED hunt (BD's `hunt_taken` and its detachment, B.16), `hiddenWaysEnabled` for the ways (dark: every body is on the road, and a hunt rides roads and countries only), `routeLifecycleEnabled` only for a way's RUNG to move (dark: the candidates alone at the hidden price, BD §5.2's degraded arm) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.16 · B.4]; belief presets carry the prediction on heard sightings, the omniscient arm on the truth in the town's land (no false trail exists there); the counterforces above; felt — "Kell's riders caught the Tolle gang on the drovers' path; the watch knows the way now" (surface), "Kell's riders lost the Tolle gang in the forest and came home" (surface), and on scrutiny the prediction's three reasons.

## §6 WHAT IT CHANGES — bounded, one truth each (items 5, 16, 17; PR5, PR6)

Every effect below is ONE lean at ONE named seam, behind `patrolEffectsEnabled` (§12); every factor DRAFT, UNMEASURED, FP-46 roster; absent the flag every host is byte-identical.

### 6.1 THE SLOW HALF — the presence subtraction (item 17(a))
**Seam:** MN §3.3's ONE composition sentence, called inside `embattlement.js :: advanceEmbattlement` before the step and living in its NEUTRAL home `spatial/presenceComposer.js :: presenceTerms` (MN §3.3: "whose home is neutral, `src/domain/spatial/presenceComposer.js :: presenceTerms`"); PR's subtraction is ONE row of that module's closed registration list (the σ slot), never a line of `embattlement.js` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W5: PR placed "PR's feeder row" in `embattlement.js` and named no module when PR-1 builds the car] — THE SHARED PRESENCE COMPOSER, an OWNED unit, MN's (MN §3.3 as REFINED in round 4 under CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d): "the composer is an OWNED unit — one manifest row, its owner volume THIS one … cut with whichever of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 is cut first, never re-implemented by a caller — and it RESOLVES THE PHYSICS, so it reads TRUTH") [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X4: the r3 "first-to-land-owns" idiom is replaced by the owned unit (CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d): "the 'first to land owns' idiom … is REPLACED by explicit ownership")]: whichever of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 is cut first carries MN's unit and its manifest row, and every other REGISTERS its feeder rows; it lights when ANY registered feeder's own flag is lit, each feeder gated by its own flag, and `menacesEnabled` gates only MN's feeder rows — so PR's subtraction needs no menace, and if C16 is cut first MN's composer unit is cut WITH PR-1 (MN's text and manifest row, never a PR re-implementation; zero kernel lines — the host receives `worldState`) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — MN-V6 (MN-r2-verify, routed to PR by A.3: "PR-1: 'inside MN's composition, which MN-1 builds'; `patrolEffectsEnabled`'s host flags omit `menacesEnabled`") — MN-1 lands at C10, after C14–C16 in the fold's order, so PR's slow half had no composer until then; MN keeps the composition's TEXT, the fold's §6 names the builder]: `presenceTerms` — per town the MAX over the feeders, capped once at `PRESENCE_TERM_CAP`, then the ONE suppressor slot (ST's sites and PR's riders, one shared cap) on the SUPPRESSIBLE part only, never on the walls term. PR does not restate that sentence; it supplies one term to the suppressor slot [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-7: three volumes restated the composition with different feeder counts; MN owns it]. **PR's term:** `patrolSubtraction(T) = PATROL_SUB_MAX × coverageOf(T)`, `PATROL_SUB_MAX` 0.15, where `coverageOf` is the mean of the town's four-week coverage ring (§2.6 — the SLOW signal, never this week's presence alone; when the levy marches, the subtraction fades over four weeks) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-25]. What is suppressible is MN's sentence (MN §3.3, cured in round 2b): `roadCountry` ONLY — never the walls term and never an enemy column (PS's feeder, widened to the destination's own land by RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 2) — so PR's riders lower a raided country's brigand-like danger and never an armed host's (MN: "an armed host is answered by the siege verdict and relief, or by the war's own contest and the screen"). A menace or an army at the walls is never suppressed; a RAIDING menace may be taken below the latch by sites and riders together — "the counterforce WORKING" (RULINGS-ROUND2b, MN's M50 wins). It lowers only what feeders ADDED, so a calm town's roads gain nothing (the host document's "peacetime roads carry no danger to relieve"). Against the latch the riders work by its own hysteresis (`embattlement.js :: stepEmbattlement`: "`} else if (rawT < T.EXIT_THRESHOLD && (now - priorSince) >= T.MIN_DWELL_TICKS) {`", `EXIT_THRESHOLD` 0.3, `MIN_DWELL_TICKS` 6): a CALM watched country may never ENTER the latch, an embattled one LEAVES only when its suppressed presence stays below 0.30 for the dwell — never while a latch-sized menace raids (its raid part less the shared cap still reads 0.35, MN M53: the level eases toward it and the town stays embattled) and never while an army is at the walls or in the land (never suppressed) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — SEAMS-r2-7 (MN-r2-verify's fail (b), PR's echo of MN5's "may fall out"): the r2b "a raided or besieged-around country slowly falls out of the embattled latch where the riders hold the roads" contradicted the landed exit law; MN's own sentence is MN's cure]. **Every danger consumer follows by construction** (§15.1): route choice (`scoreRoute`/`chooseRoute`), realized banditry (`routeDangerLevel`), the roads gauntlet's T3 and the sea's S3 (both read `embattlementLevel`), migration road deaths, charters. **Tier:** the canon marker only (`embattlementActive`); aspatial worlds have no danger arm.

### 6.2 THE COUNTER TERM — the raider row and the menace's wear (the fold's ruling, kept)
`patrolCounterTerm(T) = clamp(K_COUNTER × (coverageOf(T) − referenceCoverage(tier(T))), −0.10, +0.10)`, `K_COUNTER` 0.25 — the DEVIATION of the town's actual watch from the REFERENCE watch of its tier (the coverage a `funded` town of that tier with `internal` 60 fields at peace). **ONE entry, one value, reaching two readers:** the term enters ST's ONE additive post-blend slot after `counterforceAssessment` for `monster_raider_pressure` (ST §3.3's raider counterforce term; sites and patrols SUM inside it and take ONE clamp, ST's ±0.15) — through ST's leaf `siteTermAt`, so `stressorDynamics.js` (759) takes no PR line — and per stressor it is the FOOTPRINT MEAN of the per-town terms over `stressor.affectedSettlementIds`, the landed blend's own averaging (`counterforceAssessment`: "`const rawScore = clamp01(scoreSum / entries.length);`"), inside that one clamp [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-29]. A raiding MENACE's wear takes the term only THROUGH THE STAMP (`stressors.js :: ageRoamingStressors` stamps "`counterforce: { score: … }`", the score MN §5.5's wear read takes — MN's slot (2) "FILLED THROUGH THE STAMP, never added here"); PR adds no row to `menaceCounterTerms`, so the riders are never counted twice in a menace's wear; the one fallback is MN's — should ST-1's car-0 STOP fire, MN reads `siteTermAt`'s raider row directly instead (MN §5.5) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-7 · SEAMS-r2-4; the chair's "PR fills MN's slot (2) and ST's post-blend slot ("two consumers, one value")" (RULINGS-ROUND2b) holds in exactly this sense: one entry, the stamp carrying it to the second reader]. THE CORRIDOR: the same leaf's second row, for `criminal_corridor`, is `K_CORRIDOR × coverageOf(T)` (DRAFT 0.10, UNMEASURED) — RAW coverage, not a deviation, because the corridor's landed counterforce has NO watch term to deviate from (`STRESSOR_COUNTERFORCES.criminal_corridor`: `criminal_opportunity` (invert) 0.4 · `legitimacy` 0.3 · `labor_capacity` 0.3); PR-1's car-0 measures it [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-21]. So the riders WEAR a raiding menace and shorten ordinary raider and smuggling stressors where they ride more than a town of that size usually does, and LENGTHEN them where the watch has gone to the war, gone unpaid or been cut down ("With the watch gone to the war, the hill road has gone to the brigands" — and where a held keep stands on that road, "… the hill road beyond the keep's reach …", A.1: the keep's own rows stay). [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — R-6: the landed counterforce already reads `institution defense` 0.3 and the generated `defense_readiness` 0.5, which ARE the standing watch; adding raw coverage would count the same riders twice. The deviation adds only what the patrols DO beyond the institutions' standing presence; PR-5 measures its correlation with the landed terms (§14 PR-5) and, if ρ > 0.7 on the soak corpus, the reference narrows to the war-and-belief deviation alone.]

### 6.3 THE FAST HALF — presence this week lowers the incident roll (item 17(b))
**Seam:** `embattlement.js :: banditryLoss({ channelStrength, dangerLevel, rng, fireLevel? })` — ONE optional input: the FIRE TEST reads `fireLevel` when present (`draw >= fireLevel × BANDITRY_CHANCE`); the LOSS SIZE keeps reading `dangerLevel` (the unwatched mean — "`const frac = (T.BANDITRY_MIN_LOSS + draw2 * (…)) * danger;`"), so the fast half makes an attack rarer, never smaller; absent ⇒ byte-identical (+2 lines; host 172). **The per-hop watched level** (a leaf, `patrols/patrolEffects.js :: watchedFireLevel(route, worldState, tick)`): `fireLevel = clamp01( Σ_{i≥1} embattlementLevel(path[i]) × m_i / hops )` — hop `i` is `path[i−1] → path[i]` (the landed `scoreRoute` sum, danger per traversed node), with `m_i = 1 − WATCH_CUT × watchOn(road{path[i−1], path[i]}, tick) × (1 − slowShare(path[i]))` on a LAND hop and `m_i = 1` on a SEA hop (no naval damper — `navalLayer.js`: "no naval-patrol damper") — a per-hop multiplier on that hop's danger BEFORE the route's mean, never a route-wide MAX (RULINGS-ROUND2b PR-r2-5), so one rider on one road cuts only its own hop's share of a ten-hop route; `WATCH_CUT` 0.5 (DRAFT, UNMEASURED). **Callers** pass `fireLevel` beside `dangerLevel = routeDangerLevel(route)`: `commodityFlow.js` (+1; 474) and `supplyShipments.js` (+1; 210). **THE JOINT COMPOSITION** (item 17's "never doubled" — one composition per consumer):

| Consumer | The slow half (presence, §6.1) | The fast half (this week) | The joint bound |
|---|---|---|---|
| goods banditry (`banditryLoss`) | lowers the node level ⇒ attacks RARER and SMALLER (the size is `frac × danger`) | lowers the FIRE level on the watched hop only ⇒ RARER, never smaller | the fast cut on a hop is scaled by `1 − slowShare(node)` — `slowShare` = the share of that node's suppressible presence this week's patrol subtraction removed (the composer's own two numbers; a proxy for the ramped level, UNMEASURED) — so where the riders' slow half already holds a node their fast half adds proportionally less; THE JOINT FLOOR is an assertion PR-5 measures on the soak corpus: on a watched embattled hop the expected loss stays ≥ `JOINT_FLOOR` (DRAFT 0.25, UNMEASURED) × the same hop's expected loss with no riders; a breach lowers `WATCH_CUT`, never adds a rule |
| named travellers on roads missions (T3, S3) | through `embattlementLevel`, by construction | none — STOP-PR-1 RULED: the slow half alone now; the fast half is a roads leaf-first car on C7 | the slow half alone |
| a held fort's road effects (ST §3.3) | the keep's OWN suppression (0.20) STANDS while it is held and funded, lit or dark — its garrison is the site's own complement, never the town's riders (RULINGS-ROUND3 A.1) — and the riders' subtraction is a DISTINCT feeder of the SAME capped suppressor: `σ = min(0.25, σ_site + σ_patrol)` (MN §3.3) | the riders' own; the keep has no fast half | no fourth term and no double count (different men): the two suppressions share the ONE 0.25 cap, the keep's raider counterforce (+0.15) and the riders' deviation share ST's ONE clamp (±0.15), and the keep's three gate rows stand (PR mints none, PR5) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-r2-8 · PR-V2 (A.1): the r2b row made the keep's suppression yield to the riders while its gate rows and +0.15 stayed, half a cure] |

[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-5 (RULINGS-ROUND2b: per hop; sea hops unaffected) · PR-r2-18: one rider-week acted on one caravan's loss three ways with no joint bound; the table and the floor make it ONE composition, and PR-5 measures the joint.] **Never a write** to the embattlement level (PR6). **Named travellers** (envoys, captives, liaisons on roads missions): their T3 / S3 rolls live in the frozen `roadsKernel.js` (838, baselined) — STOP-PR-1 RULED (the slow half alone; the fast half a C7 slot). **Timing a departure** (item 17's merchant): a merchant reads `believedWatch` (§8.3) — the believed presence, never the placement — so a court that HAS riders' reports can time a caravan by them; the open road's chooser (EC-18) and WY's caravans consume the one read in their own commits.

### 6.4 THE CATCH OF GOODS — smuggling (item 16)
**Seam:** `smuggle.js :: smuggleSuccessChance({ network, corruption, goodsResistance, boldness, watch01? })` — ONE optional input at the route's WORST gate, inside `raw` BEFORE the clamp: `chance = clamp(raw − WATCH_W × watch01 × BRIBE_KEEP[pay], 0, SUCCESS_MAX) × attemptFactor(boldness)` (the landed "`return round4(clamp(raw, 0, T.SUCCESS_MAX) * attemptFactor(boldness));`"), `WATCH_W` 0.25 — so the fall is `WATCH_W × watch01 × BRIBE_KEEP × attemptFactor` where the clamp does not bind, and nothing at the floor [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-23]; `BRIBE_KEEP`: `unfunded` 0.4 · `meagre` 0.7 · `funded` 0.95 · `lavish` 1.0 — an unpaid watch takes the bribe (the wages band, the owner's sixth word's evasion; the corruption web's idiom as a lean) (+2 lines; host 119). **Caller:** `commodityFlow.js`'s M7 block passes `watch01 = watchOf({ road: road{prev(worst), worst.id}, holder: worst.id }, tick, worst.id)` — the WORST GATE TOWN's OWN riders on its half of the road into it (+1; the half-keyed read, PR-V8), never a power-blind MAX: the gate keeps the gate (J385; RULINGS-ROUND2b HOST-r2-3), so neither the destination's own riders nor an ally's raise a hostile gate's catch [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-6]. A caught shipment goes through the LANDED pipeline — `smugglePipeline`: "(detected ∧ hostile) SEIZURE → (elif contraband) CONFISCATION" with `seizureTake`'s conscience gate — never a second seizure law; the stance that makes goods contraband is EC-6's (`open · taxed · restricted · closed`, the crown's embargo one input — PL), the toll the gate's (J385: the settlement whose land a path crosses keeps the gate "with its tolls, interception and contraband"). A SMUGGLER as a PERSON (a villain's runner, CH's network) is a named traveller: §8's encounter, CH's ban/gate words on a catch, and a deed through RN's door when the catch is made (`patrol_catch` source row, §9.3). A patrol away (looking, converging) catches nothing (its stretch reads 0). A smuggler's RUN ON A HIDDEN WAY (L-BD 11b; BD §5.2: the landed `smuggler` franchise "is caught only on a KNOWN and WATCHED way (PR's trail arm)") skips the gate — its toll and its stance — and is watched ONLY by the trail arm: `watch01 = trailWatchOf(worldState, snapshot, holder, trailKey, tick)` of each holder in whose land one of the way's junctions lies (§2.6), `BRIBE_KEEP` the same; the run's path choice is EC's gauntlet's (BD's route to EC), the watch PR's (PLAUSIBLE until EC's commit names the way-run's seam) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.4's one seam, PR's half of L-BD 11b]. **THE UNPAID-WAGES SLOT (item 16) is also a GANG's mark:** THE BODIES' gang choice `bribe` (BD §4.2: "a watch whose wages go unpaid (THE PATROLS' item 16 slot: PR's riders unpaid …)") targets this slot — the town's riders' PAY band below `funded` (`unfunded` or `meagre`: `BRIBE_KEEP` 0.4 · 0.7, read from ST's `settlementFundingBand`, §3.1) — and its effect is BD's alone: the court's stance one step toward `tolerated` at its next `body_stance` fork and the gang's standing there (BD §4.2, §7.3) — NEVER a second cut on PR's notice, whose stance factor already reads the court's stance (§8.1) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD→PR route (11) (`cure/D-BD-r4.md` §5: "the warden's `bribe` on the unpaid-wages slot")]. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — R-4: the brief's "the roads gauntlet's INTERCEPTION odds … the landed contraband outcome" — measured, the gauntlet rolls hazards for MISSIONS only; goods are intercepted in `smuggle.js` at the worst gate, which is where the riders' lean lands.]

### 6.5 THE WITNESS (PR10) — `witnessedCrossing`
`witnessedCrossing(worldState, holderId, gateKey, unit, tick) = holderEyesOf(worldState, snapshot, { road: gateKey, holder: holderId }, tick) > 0 (the holder's EYES on its own half of the gate's road this week — its own riders, or a net rider standing there by right, PR10 — the placement; the half-keyed read, PR-V8) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X3: the r4 term read the holder's own riders only, against PR10's net arm] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-6 · SEAMS-r2-8: the compiled "`watchOn(…)` has the holder's power" was a type mismatch] ∨ the crossing's place is the holder's seat (`settlement:<B>`: a landing or a circle arrival) ∨ sizeBand(unit) ∈ {pressing, decisive} ∨ siteWitnesses(worldState, stretch) ∋ holderId (a held watchtower, ST17)` for a road or seat place; for a TRAIL place `trail:<a|b>` it is `trailWatchOf(worldState, snapshot, holderId, trailKey, tick) > 0` ALONE (the holder KNOWS the way — `knownTrailsOf`, B.13 — and its eyes stand on one of the way's junction halves this week) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — the PS→PR routes (`cure/D-PS-r4.md` §5): the trail arm and the seat arm (PS8's round-4 bracket)]. With `patrolsEnabled` dark it answers `true` (PS8's conservative arm). Witnessing is TRUTH (a structural fact of whether the holder saw it); what the holder believes about the unit's purpose stays PS3's claim-and-belief law. A watched stretch defeats the quiet crossing; a grouped detachment on a stretch notices what a lone rider might miss only through §8.1's notice odds (for persons), never for an army (an army is seen when it is met).

### 6.6 THE DRAW (PR3) — `patrolDraw`
**Seam: the opener, BEFORE its target loop** (RULINGS-ROUND2b PR-r2-1, completed by RULINGS-ROUND3 B PR-V1). In `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` (685 eff) the court's capacity is read once — "`const fromCap = capacityFor(fromId);`" — and then judged TWICE before any seed: the coalition gate ("`classifyFeasibility({ attackerCurrent: fromCap.offensive, …`") and, inside the solo target loop, the `CONQUEST_MARGIN` prefilter ("`fromStrength * casusMult * mediation <= strengthFor(targetId) + CONQUEST_MARGIN`") and the hard gate ("`attackerCurrent: fromCap.offensive`"); only then is the record seeded ("`seedDeploymentState({ targetId: chosenTarget, cap: fromCap, …`"). So the draw is taken RIGHT AFTER `fromCap` — ONE host line, `const drawn = patrolDrawnCap(worldState, snapshot, fromId, fromCap)` (+1; the riders kept depend on the envelope and the court's posture, never on the target) — and the four reads take the drawn army in place, with no further lines: both `classifyFeasibility` calls read `drawn.cap.offensive`, the prefilter reads `fromStrength × drawn.drawShare01` (`fromStrength` is `buildStrengthLookup`'s `settlementStrength` index, a different scale, so the draw enters it as the same SHARE; PLAUSIBLE that the index scales with `offensive`, PR-5 measures), and the seed reads `cap: drawn.cap`. Inside the leaf: `points = Math.min(patrolDrawOf(worldState, snapshot, fromId, kept), fromCap.offensive)` — the strength ACTUALLY drawn; `drawn.cap = points > 0 ? { ...fromCap, offensive: fromCap.offensive − points } : fromCap` (the same object when nothing is drawn); `drawShare01 = drawn.cap === fromCap ? 1 : (fromCap.offensive > 0 ? drawn.cap.offensive / fromCap.offensive : 0)` — 1 whenever nothing was drawn, the 0/0 guard reading 0 (unreachable once `points > 0` implies a positive offensive), a draw that takes the whole army reading 0 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W3 (`skeptic/PR-r3-verify.md`, EXECUTED there: `drawShare01 = drawnCap.offensive / fromCap.offensive` is `NaN` when `offensive` is 0 and riders are kept, "so the prefilter never skips"; RULINGS-ROUND4 B: "`drawShare01` guards 0/0 as 0")] [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — the share is taken over the points ACTUALLY drawn, so a court with no offensive to draw from reads 1 and keeps the landed prefilter: EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r4-probe/drawshare.out` — the hard gate admits a zero-offensive attacker against a collapsing defender ("attacker 0 · defender 40 · legitimacy 10 → require_betrayal · permits siege true", `feasibilityGate.js :: classifyFeasibility`'s collapse override precedes every floor), so a literal 0 on an undrawn zero army would skip a target the dark arm keeps although no rider was drawn; the chair's 0 holds for every draw that takes the whole army ("offensive 5 · draw 12 → … drawShare01 0"), and the stamp's `points` never exceed the strength drawn — the alternative (the literal 0/0 → 0 with `points` uncapped) is in §19]. The seeded record carries the conditional `patrolDraw: { riders, points }` stamp by ONE line beside the seed, written WHENEVER RIDERS ARE KEPT — `points` 0 where there was nothing to draw (+1, PR-W2; §2.7) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X5: a court with no offensive that kept riders carried no stamp, so the kept count that bounds `fielded` while its army is out had no carrier]. The `HOSTILE_CONFIDENCE` gate before `fromCap` keeps the undrawn index (the court's confidence in its whole strength, before it decides what to keep). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V1: the r2b draw sat at the seed, after both gates had judged the undrawn army — a metropolis keeping nine `capable` riders marched 27 latent points below what its gate judged feasible, more than one band (floors 78/60/42/26); the alternative "an optional argument to `seedDeploymentState`" is withdrawn, since the seed comes after the gates.] `start` — and with it `maxStartStrength` and `currentEffectiveStrength`, the stateful strength `resolveSiegeVerdict` reads ("`const eff = Number.isFinite(stateful) ? … (stateful) : cap.offensive;`") — is the army without the riders the town kept. The per-tick `offensive` and `homeDefense` of `buildCapacityLookup` are NEVER touched (the kept riders are home, and the walls keep them); the draw is re-read only at a RE-DEPLOY (a new seed); the light-record migration (`ensureStatefulRecord`) seeds no draw — it is no decision ("the sizing DECISION is at the new-deploy site, not a migration"). `patrolDrawOf` = `DRAW_PER_BAND[band] × kept riders` (DRAFT: `thin` 1 · `modest` 2 · `capable` 3 latent points each; a city keeping six `modest` riders marches an army twelve points weaker — under one strength band). By construction the opener's feasibility gates and prefilter, the siege verdict and the attrition ledger read the drawn army (the gates through `drawn.cap`, the rest through the seed); allied relief reads the reliever's FIELD FORCE `homeDefense − siteDefense` (RULINGS-ROUND3 §E.3, RULINGS-ROUND4 A.5; OD-PR-41's one composition), untouched by the draw [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V1: "the chooser's weighing of its own army read the seed" was false — the gates ran before the seed] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W4: "allied relief reads `homeDefense`" contradicted OD-PR-41 and §E.3's field force]. Dark ⇒ the copy is never made and the stamp is absent: byte-identical. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-1 (BLOCKING): the compiled draw on the per-tick `offensive` never reached the army (seeded at deploy "regardless of mode", the siege reading the stateful strength) and always reached the walls (`homeDefense = offensive − armyAway`) — the owner's trade-off inverted.]

### 6.7 What patrols NEVER change
The stressor BIRTH gates (PR5; the landed rows are the standing watch) · the per-tick `offensive` and `homeDefense` (the draw is at deploy, §6.6) · `menaceCounterTerms` (the stamp carries the term, §6.2) · a sea hop's roll (no naval damper, §6.3) · the ramp's security relief · the embattlement level directly · `defenseLedger` (read-only; its importers include the frozen transit kernel) · the map's sway or its drawn danger (the map's law: "A patrol is never drawn lowering danger" stands for the MAP — the map draws the host's danger; the host's riders lower the host's danger because the host is the truth) · a plague's spread (no patrol slows a plague; a quarantine is the host's verb, §9.2(f)) · any named person's fate.

## §7 THE COUNTERFORCES (requirement 2) — every positive names its price

### 7.1 Wages and men
Riders cost: the PAY gate shrinks them with the town's prosperity (§3.1) — and, when W-COIN lights, coin through the treasury's `patrols` upkeep row (the slot); the POOL is the levy's own men, so riders kept on the roads while the army marches are a weaker field army (`patrolDraw`, §6.6). An UNPAID watch is few, thin, and takes bribes (§6.4's `BRIBE_KEEP`); an unpaid garrison is how a fort becomes a den (ST item 11(a): abandonment by want).

### 7.2 Loss — AMBUSH and the stronger body
A patrol or detachment that meets a body stronger than its band can be CUT DOWN (§11.2 `patrol_engages` → `cut_down`): a `losses` row (the count drops for a season — the recovery, §2.7), a DEED for the NAMED body that did it — RN's `DEED_SOURCES` row `patrol_clash` (§9.3), DOER THE BODY (`{ kind: 'body', id }`, keyed `g:<bodyId>` for a gang, its creed's `creed:<deityRef>` for a conclave; a beast and a nameless body are never doers, RN §2.1, so none), its leading paragon riding as `ledBy` (CH's `paragon.leads` at the act's tick — the one leader record, B.12) — ONE doer per deed and never also a `body_act` (RN §3.1: "ONE ACT, ONE DEED … a patrol's clash with a body is PR's `patrol_clash` … never a `body_act` beside them"); a detachment cut down INSIDE a LED hunt mints no `patrol_clash` (the mission's close is the one door, B.5 — the body's `escaped` where the hunt fails) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes: the "band's deed" slot is THE BODIES' key] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RN→PR route (`cure/D-RN-r4.md` §5: "doer the body (`g:`/its creed; a beast none), its leading paragon riding as `ledBy`. This keeps one doer per deed. A clash is `patrol_clash`, never also a `body_act`") · B.5: the r4 "or the villain, where RN admits the source (BD's `body_act`)" named a second doer and a second door], and its rumor ("Kell's riders fell at the ford"); a LED detachment cut down loses riders, never its leader (§5.5: no named fate); NO grievance input of its own — the commons' one registered input from PR is §7.5's, and a lost patrol's felt cost is the deed, the tale and the season without riders [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-28: a second, unregistered grievance input ("§7.5's input, sign reversed") is struck]. No population is debited: a patrol's dead are below the census's grain — WC's people ledger owns headcounts when it lands (the slot), and the `losses` row is the fact. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — a recorded decision (macro over micro); vetoable: the alternative debits the town's population through the landed sink functions (SMN-04's idiom), a same-seed shift at a negligible size.]

### 7.3 Fatigue — derived, never a stock
A detachment out more than `REST_BOUND` weeks (DRAFT 4) sends its riders `resting` for one week before they return to the round (a receipt); in wartime the riders' quality falls with the town's war exhaustion (`warExhaustion[id]`, the landed scar: `quality × (1 − FATIGUE_W × exhaustion01)`, `FATIGUE_W` 0.3). No fatigue stock exists, and no second decay law is minted (requirement 2's counterforce is read from the war's own scar and the detachment's own `weeksOut`). [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — R-12: the brief's "a patrol's readiness wears on the beat and rests at home — a band" as a per-patrol band would need per-patrol identity across weeks and a decaying stock; derived from `weeksOut` and the war's scar it needs neither.]

### 7.4 False reports
A town's riders go where its belief sends them (§5.1), so a planted or staged report (IN's plant road; CH's provocateur) pulls riders off their round until the look finds nothing; the round's lowest stretches go unwatched meanwhile (§4.1 step 4 draws from the bottom of the round). The deceiver's road is IN's and CH's; PR prices the effect by construction.

### 7.5 Over-patrolling — the harassment the commons feel
THE TRIGGER IS THE FORK, never "`fielded` at `max`" (RULINGS-ROUND3 B PR-V3): when, in PEACE with nothing believed near, the court's own PEACE-POSTURE fork has held `peaceShare > PEACE_SHARE` for one season (§3.2: a wary court, a lawful-cruel seat) — `tick − forkSince ≥ INTERVAL_WEEKS.one_season` (`worldPulse/intervalWeeks.js :: INTERVAL_WEEKS`, "`one_season: 13,`"), where `forkSince` (§2.7) is the first week of the CURRENT unbroken run of such weeks, written by the stage at the run's first week, kept while the run holds and DELETED the week it breaks (war, a believed threat, the share at or below `PEACE_SHARE`, the town fielding none) — or the DM's standing `PATROL_ORDER` (`first` on `round`) has held one season (`tick − orders.setTick ≥ one_season`), the commons feel watched [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W1 (MAJOR; `skeptic/PR-r3-verify.md`: "Nothing persists when it began"): the r3 trigger had no carrier — CONFIRMED by reading master: `commonsVoiceKernel.js :: commonsGrievance01` is a pure per-call blend ("`return clamp01(CV.W_LEGIT * legitDeficit + CV.W_CORRUPT * … + CV.W_UNREST * …);`") and `strategicPosture.js :: courtRiskAppetiteOf` returns "`{kind, id, present, stock01, band, receipt}`", no since-tick; the owner's "held for a season" intent is kept by ONE declared field on the placement row (OD-PR-44, the owner's shape) rather than restated away] — ONE registered input into the commons' ONE grievance home, `commonsVoiceKernel.js :: commonsGrievance01` ("THE ONE HOME of the commons grievance blend"), sized by the riders the fork or the order ACTUALLY ADDS: `W_HARASS × clamp01(excess / (max − ceil(max × PEACE_SHARE)))`, `excess = fielded − ceil(max × PEACE_SHARE)`, zero whenever `excess ≤ 0`; `W_HARASS` 0.10 (DRAFT, UNMEASURED; PLAUSIBLE until PR-1's car-0 measures the input's registration path in that kernel and its `commonsVoiceEnabled` gate) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V3: with `fielded = ceil(max × peaceShare)` and the share ≥ 0.4, a one-rider town always stood "at `max`" (EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r3-probe/arith.out` (2): 15 of 60 rows, 3 of them by rounding alone, the boldest court among them), so every hamlet fed the grievance forever and "a good seat … keeps the commons quiet" was false] [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the excess gate: the bare fork would grieve a wary one- or two-rider town that fields EXACTLY the riders a median court of its size fields (`harass.out`: 6 of the fork's 36 rows), two identical pictures with two grievances — incoherent on scrutiny; sized by the riders added, the input fires in 30 rows and only where the riders differ]. **The alignment lens** (requirement 13): the fork itself carries it — a lawful-cruel seat's `harsh01` (`settlementAlignment`: `lawfulness01` × `malice01`) lifts its peace share toward `max` and the grievance follows (it keeps the watch and pays the grumbling gladly, since the fork never counts the grievance); a good seat adds nothing through `harsh01`, so only its own wariness (a low risk appetite) can lift its share and the grumbling with it — a good court with a bold or median appetite keeps the commons quiet; the DM's order pays the same price by the riders it adds, said on the card ("the commons grumble at riders on every road") [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V3: the false claim is restated] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-27: the "chooser" this lens named did not exist; it is §3.2's fork]. **The hunt among a body's friends** (THE BODIES §7.6, on the owner's 18:43 word "Go with your common's stance toward a boy"): the SAME one registered input takes a SECOND ARM — a week in which a `pursue` detachment rides in a town's own country after a body whose commons stance there is `support` (BD's `commonsStanceOf`) feeds `W_HARASS × clamp01(members / max)`, the hunters against the town's envelope — and the input reads the GREATER of its two arms, never their sum (one input, one weight); BD §7.6: "a HUNT there costs the court the commons' grievance (THE PATROLS' over-policing term `W_HARASS`, PR §7.5 — cite, never a second)" [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD §7.6's claim on PR carried, so the two texts agree; vetoable: the alternative leaves such a hunt free and BD strikes its clause]. [PLAUSIBLE — the grievance input's exact host line is measured at PR-1's car-0.]

### 7.6 The crown's riders over a lord's head — the grudge
Riders of the banner on a lord's roads OVER ITS HEAD are PL's PRECEDENCE FRICTION (PL §4.7 F.6 (b) as cured in round 5): ONE `riders_quartered` ROW on that lord's own sworn edge, carrying ONE resentment patch (a grip term, so its deference falls, PL §4.9) — PL's own row, the OBLIGATION family, NEVER a weave incident (PL §15.9 no longer lists it) — in two arms: (i) THE SWEEP — riders of the banner not the lord's own PLACED on its road halves in a season in which no cover occasion stood for it (its own riders home, PL F.4), read at the season's end from the one SEASON TALLY (`bannerCoverOf(…).weeks > 0`, §2.6), whatever need placed them (the crown's war roads and trade, its peace call, or a threat the lord's own riders could not reach — the help is real, and so is the precedence; a lord that wants the help without the grudge ASKS, `patrol_joins`, §10.1 (d)), at most one a season (episode `riders_quartered:<rootId>:<seasonStartTick>`); (ii) THE RIDE PAST — the crown's direct summons under `unbroken` that took a member's men past a lord it did not summon, or that refused or let the call lapse (PL §4.1 (ii); §10.3 (b)), at most one per lord per call; the resentment is the hold's own counterforce (PL's `D̄`). A season the banner's riders COVERED a member whose own riders went to the levy is PROTECTION (§10.3 (f): PL's `protection_kept`), never friction [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7 (RULINGS-ROUND3 F.6: "resents an overlord who sweeps his roads unasked"; F.4: the protection is "recorded as a deed")] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND5 A.8 ("F.6 (b) prices the sweep as the RESENTMENT PATCH, not `riders_quartered` wear") · STOP-PR-r4-1's ride-past arm · the PL→PR route (`cure/D-PL-r5.md` §5: "`riders_quartered` is now PL's shape-3 ROW with ONE resentment patch (the sweep once a season where the lord's own riders stayed home; the ride past once per call), no longer a weave incident"): the r4 text priced the sweep as weave wear, which is lit nowhere, and excused a threat need from it, which PL's one tally cannot tell apart]. A WEAK hold sends them home to the ladder (an ally's patrol on a member's road, §10.3 (d)); a BROKEN rung makes them a foreign unit (§10.3 (g)).
