## §8 TRAVELLERS AND PATROLS — the encounter, the catch by kind, and planning on belief (the owner's fifth word; items 15, 16)

### 8.1 THE ONE ENCOUNTER LAW — `patrolEncounter`
For a named traveller on road half `h` (the half its LEG's `progress01` places it on against the gate's own fraction along the leg, §2.2 — PR-V8) in week `t`, ONCE PER POWER whose riders stand on `h` that week (the placement) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-6: with an ally and a hostile holder on one road, a power-blind MAX dropped one stance]:
- **MET** (truth; PR4): `watchOf(h, t, power) > 0` — that power's riders on the traveller's OWN half `h` that week, never the whole road [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V8: the r2b MET tested `road(h)`, so a traveller on the origin's half met riders standing on the far half]; `by` names the power (the holder of the land, an ally, the crown). A traveller of the power's OWN town is never noticed by its own riders — they are its escort, not its watch [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-11].
- **NOTICED** (a keyed draw, stream `patrol:notice:<travellerKey>:<roadKey>:<power>:<t>`): `p = clamp(NOTICE_BASE × watch01 × wartime × stance × tower × (1 − cover), 0, NOTICE_CAP)` (every factor DRAFT, UNMEASURED; `NOTICE_BASE` 0.5, `NOTICE_CAP` 0.9):
  - `cover` — the traveller's own: a paragon's FAME AT THE PATROL'S TOWN read backwards (RN's `fameIn(worldState, actorRef, power, tick).scope` — fame as that town's BELIEF, never `fameOf`'s world-wide truth: `realm` −0.4 cover, `regional` −0.2; a famous enemy captain is recognised only where the riders' town has heard of him) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-12]; NO cover-identity term exists (no landed or designed "merchant's face": ES's only covert skill is `covertCompetence01(home, npc)`, ES §3.2 — the home's criminal power and activity, damped by the operative's notoriety) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V4: the r2b "+0.3" cover identity cited an ES class that does not exist]; a SPY's competence enters ONCE, in ES's own form, in the spy's row of §8.2 (never twice, PR-r2-9);
  - `stance` — the patrol's town toward the traveller's home, from the patrol's town's belief by the landed idiom: `b = belief(worldState, power, home)`; `label = b.source === 'belief' ? b.record.allianceLabel : b.source === 'truth' ? <the true label> : 'unknown'` (unknown reads as strangers): `hostile` 1.5 · strangers 1.0 · `allied` 0.5; a standing BAN on the traveller (the landed `ExclusionEdge`, widened to actor refs — RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH §4) × 2 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-22: `belief` returns `{ source, record? }`, the label lives on `record`];
  - `wartime` — the patrol's town at war with anyone: × 1.3 (everyone is questioned);
  - `tower` — a held watchtower on the road (ST17): × 1.3;
  - quality is already inside `watch01` (band, pay, fatigue); a DETACHMENT on the half reads its summed band (a grouped detachment notices more).
- **AN ENVOY ANNOUNCES ITSELF:** for an envoy `noticed = met` (cover 0 means seen — no draw), and what follows is gated by the riders' town's rung toward the envoy's court (PS §6.5's default): `ally` → PASSED, no card ("a friend's envoy is not an engagement") · stranger → ONE card on PS's ENVOY ARM, default `escorted` · enemy → the same card, default `turned_back` (the arm's own line `stopped · escorted · turned_back`; PS's ledger module mints it, PR's stage calls the mint) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-11: "always noticed" was false under the draw (at most 0.75 × the watch for a hostile court in peace), and an allied envoy was routed to interception against item 15(a), "allied → passed"].
- **A BODY ON THE ROAD, ON A TRAIL, IN THE COUNTRY** (THE BODIES — a gang, a conclave, a beast; BD's route (1)) is a traveller ITS OWN STAGE moves (`bodies/bodyStage.js :: advanceBodies` calls this law after the riders are placed, one call per power whose riders stand at its place — BD §2.7). MET (truth): on a road half, as any traveller; on a hidden way `trail:<a|b>` (BD §5.2), ONLY where the power KNOWS the way and its EYES stand on one of the way's junction halves that week — `trailWatchOf` (§2.6), ZERO on a way it does not know; in a country or at a site, where a detachment stands there (a look, a hunt, a clearing). NOTICED: the draw above with BD's COVER term as `cover` — on a way its `cover01` from THE BODIES' `hiddenWayEdges`, the ONE read over the ONE ladder (master's hidden rung, B.4); on a road its size's visibility, BD's `ROAD_COVER_BY_MIGHT` (BD §9.1: "`thin` 0.5 · `modest` 0.3 · `capable` 0.15 · `strong`/`formidable` 0, DRAFT, UNMEASURED: a handful can pass for travellers, a war band cannot"); PLUS BD's commons term, +0.2 (DRAFT) wherever the body stands in the country of a town whose COMMONS SUPPORT it (BD §7.6: "its COVER in its town's country rises (one bounded term on BD's cover input to THE PATROLS' encounter law, DRAFT +0.2, PR §8.1)"; `commonsStanceOf` `support`) — the sum clamped to [0, 1] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.4 (BD's r5 route (a): "the encounter's `cover01` reads BD's `hiddenWayEdges` on the ONE ladder … no second graph") · BD's r5 route (e) (`ROAD_COVER_BY_MIGHT`) · BD→PR route (8) (`cure/D-BD-r4.md` §5: "the commons' support as +0.2 cover in the body's town country")] — and `stance` read from the power's COURT STANCE toward the body (BD's `courtStanceOf`, never a label of a pair the body has none of): `hunted` · `outlawed` · a beast 1.5 · `curbed` · `tolerated` 1.0 · `permitted` · `endowed` · `employed` · `harboured` 0 (the riders let it be — the owner's good conclave "not … pursued by patrols"). Met and noticed ⇒ for a pursued stance or a beast, `patrol_engages` (§11.2; THE HUNT's catch, §5.5); for `curbed` or `tolerated`, a `patrol_report` `crossing_seen` naming the body (the town's belief sharpens; no card); and a body that SEEKS a patrol it outmatches (the owner's word 14: "maybe sometimes patrols where they deem it appropriate"; BD14) opens the same `patrol_engages` as the aggressor, whatever the stance [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (1) (the BODY row and BD's cover term) · (4) (trail coverage zero unless known) · (5) (pursue only `hunted` · `outlawed` and beasts)].
- **TWO BODIES** (BD's route (2); BD §9.1: "two bodies in the same place in the same week … meet through THE PATROLS' ONE encounter law WIDENED to body × body") — the law's SECOND WATCHER is a body: MET is co-location (truth: the same country, stretch, trail or site's attachment in the same week); `watch01` reads 1; the factors are BD's — the watcher's SEEK (a `feud` choice, a `hunt` stance toward a beast, hunger up; loot carried down) in place of `wartime × stance × tower`, the other body's cover as above; each side draws once (stream `bodies_meet:notice:<pairKey>:<watcherId>:<t>`, the digest's `pairKey` form); unnoticed by both they pass and nothing is minted; noticed by either, the card is BD's `bodies_meet` (BD's kind, BD's vocabulary). One law, two watchers; PR moves no body and hears no body-to-body meeting [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (2)].
- **A PERSON OR A RUN ON A WAY** (CH §5's trail arm; L-BD 11b · 11c): a PARAGON whose leg takes a known way (CH OD-CH-42: "a known trail is taken at the landed person price `HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN` and met by `trailWatchOf`"), a covert envoy routed on its court's known ways (L-BD 11c; the landed `covert_envoy` franchise), a smuggler's run (§6.4) — any traveller master's hidden franchise admits — is MET there ONLY as a body is: where the power knows the way and its eyes stand on one of the way's junction halves (`trailWatchOf`, §2.6), ZERO on a way it does not know; NOTICED by the draw above, the way's `cover01` ADDED to the traveller's own cover (clamped to [0, 1]); and the catch by kind is §8.2's owner's, exactly as on the road (ES's `spy_at_target` at WHERE `road (a, b)` with the way named on the card, CH's gate words, the smuggling pipeline) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — CH→PR route 3 (`cure/D-CH-r4.md` §6: "a PARAGON on a trail is met by `trailWatchOf` exactly as a body is") · B.4's one seam; the way's cover added to a person's REFINED, vetoable].
- A patrol AWAY (`looking`, `converging`) is not on the half: it notices nothing.

ONE law, named at its seam; the consumers below call it at THEIR movers; PR moves no traveller.

### 8.2 WHAT A CATCH IS, BY KIND — each owner's ONE road, never a second law
| Traveller | Its mover (the consumer that calls §8.1) | Noticed ⇒ | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| a SPY on a covert errand (ES) | ES's in-transit errand pass | ONE ROAD EXPOSURE, the patrolled road as ES's third exposure class: PR's notice IS the road's catch roll — for a spy `p = clamp(NOTICE_BASE × watch01 × wartime × stance × tower × (1 − SHELTER_W · covertCompetence01(home, npc)), 0, CATCH_CAP)`, ES's competence entering ONCE in ES's own form (`SHELTER_W`, `CATCH_CAP`) and the stance once (PR's, by belief); ES's `catch01` is NOT run on the road (its terms — `orderFactor`, `porosity`, `underways`, `legStack`, `dwellRamp` — are the hostile TOWN's and have no road meaning). Noticed = `caught`: the outcome and the taint cascade are ES's — the landed `spy_at_target` kind with WHERE `road` (`caught · uncaught`; RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 3, RULED). ES §3.3's J-ES-1 ("Two exposure classes, BY CONSTRUCTION of the host … no bandits, no sea lanes, no ambient risk"; "an exposure event, not a patrol event") gains an amendment bracket for the owner's fifth word ("spies crossing paths with a patrol unit have an appropratie chance of getting caught") — the fold lane's, per the chair [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-9: competence and stance were each counted twice when the noticed spy entered `catch01`] | ES |
| an ENVOY in hostile land (the errand spine) | the errand spine's in-transit pass | STOPPED only as a ROAD HEARING — THE ENVOY ARM of PS's `passage_at_the_border` (PS §6.5), WHERE `road{a, b}`, with its own closed line `stopped · escorted · turned_back` and no mover line (an envoy is never a passage mover, PS1), its default by the rung (ally: no card · stranger `escorted` · enemy `turned_back`) — NEVER `envoy_intercepted` / `envoy_held`: those are the ARMY interception's (`envoyEncounter.js`: "Exact node equality is the whole collision law"; `foreignGuestHold.js :: normalizeForeignGuestHold` drops any hold without an `encounterId`), and an envoy HELD by a patrol waits on ES-2b, the owner's slot (RULINGS-ROUND2 A.11's class); PS's ledger module is the card's one writer and PR's stage calls its mint; the engine never harms an envoy [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-10 · SEAMS-r2-2 (BLOCKING), RULINGS-ROUND2b] | PS |
| an ENEMY PARAGON on its leg (CH) | THE ONE PERSON STAGE (CH's weekly pass) | recognised and TURNED BACK: CH's town's-gate words `refused` (`person_at_the_gate`'s vocabulary) at the road — on a known way met ONLY by the trail arm (§8.1) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — CH→PR route 3]; SEIZED only when custody lands (ES-2b, the owner's slot; RULINGS-ROUND2 A.11 — no engine custody until then) | CH |
| a liaison's run, a villain's runner (CH's network) | the same | CH's gate words; the corruption web's exposure (`corruptionWeb.js :: applyForeignExposureBlowback`, source `covert_hire_exposed`) when the runner carries a clandestine hire | CH |
| the PARTY (PT) | PT's journey (the DM's toggle moving the party along a route) | PT's `party_gate` WIDENED to WHERE `road (a, b)`, fired by a patrolled traversal of the DM's own move — CH's words unchanged (`admitted · refused · admitted_under_watch · struck`), no new kind (STOP-PR-2 RULED, RULINGS-ROUND2b; PT's row and PR's cite) — its default "nothing has happened to the party yet"; a court's `party_ambush` laid on the road sends a DETACHMENT of its riders as PT's WATCH (PR19); a `party_warrant` stands at the court's town, never on a road (RULINGS-ROUND3 B, PT's row) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — STOP-PR-2 · PR-r2-13] | PT |
| a caravan (WY / EC-18) | WY-6's caravan encounter | WY-6's `caravan_seized` (`seized · ransomed · passed`) at the patrol's stance — a hostile patrol may seize; an escorted caravan's seizure odds fall (§10.4) | WY |
| goods (aggregate shipments) | `commodityFlow.js` M7 | §6.4's `smuggle.js` pipeline | EC / TR |
| a military unit (PS) | PS's transit leaf | `witnessedCrossing` (§6.5) | PS |
| a CORPS (CH's mark on a UNIT; RN keys it `u:<unitKey>`, RULINGS-ROUND2b SEAMS-r2-6) | its unit's own mover | a military unit: PS's witness and ladder, as any unit [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the corps ruling reaches the riders through PS; PR mints nothing for it] | PS |
| a BODY — a gang, a conclave, a beast (THE BODIES; never a passage mover, PS1) | BD's stage (`advanceBodies`, BD §2.7), on the road, a trail (MET only if the power knows it — `trailWatchOf`) or in the country | by the court's stance toward it (§8.1): `patrol_engages` for a `hunted` gang, an `outlawed` conclave, a beast, or a body seeking the riders; a `crossing_seen` report for `curbed` or `tolerated`; nothing for `permitted`, `endowed`, `employed`, `harboured`. A catch on a trail teaches the town the trail (`by_catch`, BD's writer) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (1)] | PR (the card) · BD (the body) |

**Nuances kept as rows (item 15(d)):** a spy from a home with a strong underworld passes a routine stop more often (ES's `covertCompetence01`, stance `stranger`); the same spy at war, on a watched road, under a tower, does not (× 1.3 × 1.3, cover only its competence) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V4]; a grouped detachment notices more (its band); a patrol away notices nothing.

### 8.3 PLANNING IS BELIEF — ONE believed-watch read for every planner
Before any journey the traveller's OWN court plans on what it BELIEVES about the roads ahead: `believedWatch(worldState, snapshot, observerId, roadKey, tick)` — never the foreign town's truth. ITS PRODUCERS, named [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-16: the compiled read had no producer and an undefined `null`]: (1) its OWN riders at a shared gate see the neighbour's riders on the neighbour's half — a `patrol_report` with `finding: 'riders_seen'` (§9.3; PR's own producer); (2) its ALLIES' riders' reports, exchanged at the gate (§10.1(b)); (3) its TRAVELLERS' RETURN — a named traveller of its own that met (or did not meet) riders on a road carries the fact home on its owner's carried-picture idiom (the errand spine's envoy picture, RF-6; a caravan's factor) — one watched/unwatched row per road crossed, a registration in each owner's commit (PLAUSIBLE until each owner's car-0 measures its picture's room); (4) ES's PRODUCT — a spy at a foreign town reports the watch on its roads (ES's product row, ES's commit); (5) RUMOR of a clash or a catch on the road (a travelling `patrol_report`). Each belief ages toward the prior over the rumor's own TTL; with nothing heard the read returns the TIER-REFERENCE PRIOR of the road's holder with `known: false`, and the card says "unknown". ONE read, consumed in each planner's commit (a registration, never a second planning law): ES's mission planning (the dispatch refusal already reads BELIEVED danger, ES HZ9), the errand spine's route, CH's leg choice, EC-18's open road, WY's caravans (timing a departure, §6.3), THE PASSAGE's planner (the junction stretch of a trail the mover knows — a registration in PS-4's commit, the PS→PR route) and THE BODIES' route choice (BD12: "through THE PATROLS' ONE believed-watch read") — for which the read takes a TRAIL key too (the believed watch of the trail's junction where the body believes the holder knows the trail — a catch it heard of there, a warden it has heard holds the holder's office — else 0), returns the BELIEVED PHASE of a periodic half (`phase`, §2.3's learnable clock — "ridden every four weeks → raid in between", L-BD 39), and is BANNER-BLIND: a body composes every power's riders it has heard of on the road, whoever's they are ("A BODY sees one banner … deterrence by belief; never the ownership", RULINGS-ROUND3 F.4) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (7) · L-BD 39 · L-BD 41 (b)]. PT does NOT consume it — the DM sees all, and PT's reachability card is the DM's read, not a court's belief (PT's own patrols line) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-24]. What a planner does with a believed-watched road: AVOID it (the route re-scored with the believed watch as a cost beside danger — a `scoreRoute`-shaped term in the planner's own leaf, DRAFT weight), go ROUND it (by a hidden way — master's hidden rung, read through THE BODIES' `hiddenWayEdges` (B.4), open only to the movers master's franchise admits — `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS`' covert envoy, smuggler and wanderer, and by declared changes a body and, in war, a small unit (BD §5.2) — at the landed hidden price ×2 (a beast ×1), size-capped at `modest`, met only at a junction of a way the holder knows; never a caravan, which takes a longer road at its weeks) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route: the slot "the hidden-road class when it lands — the host document's M4 stub" is filled] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.4: the slot is master's hidden rung, BD's derivation only the candidate geometry (the map's canon overriding it when supplied, BD20); the BD quote of the r4 text no longer exists], or RISK it in haste (urgency, receipted — "the envoy would not wait"). Wrong belief sends an envoy into a patrol its court never knew of; a deceiver can make a road LOOK watched or empty (IN's plant road). The card says which in words ("Kell's envoy took the hill road; Kell did not know Ashford's riders were on it").

**The road catch's vocabulary** is ES's landed `caught · uncaught` — the `spy_at_target` kind with WHERE `road` (RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 3, RULED); the fold lane brackets ES §3.3's J-ES-1 for the owner's fifth word [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2b Q3].

## §9 FIRST RESPONDERS AND FIRST TO LOOK (the owner's third and fourth words; items 13, 14)

### 9.1 When war breaks out (item 13)
A war "breaks out" for a town the week any of these is first true: a live war front naming it (a declaration), an enemy army witnessed in its land (§6.5; PS's crossing rows) or on a stretch of its round, PS's `armed_incursion` opened in its land, a menace believed approaching (MN's court picture, §5.1).
- **(a) NOTICE.** The riders' `patrol_report` is the town's FIRST BELIEF of the war's shape — believed size (the army's band as witnessed), direction, the gate crossed — a hop earlier than the war's own front reads; it is the war's FIRST-VISIBLE sign in that town (requirement 16), and it lights PS's ONE fourth readiness signal `armsNear` on the town a week early (`martialReadiness.js :: threatEnvironment01`; PR is that signal's second producer, never a fifth signal) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's "feeds the readiness term (`raidHot`)": `raidHot` is built from war-type STRESSORS' affected towns; a believed approaching host is PS's `armsNear` in kind].
- **(b) SCREEN AND DELAY — an army marching on the riders' OWN town, or crossing its land toward another.** A detachment with aim `screen` meets the van on a half of the round. The van does NOT stand for the screen's hearing (unlike PS's border hearing, the riders are not the holder's gate answering a request): the card is heard in its week, and its VERDICT applies the screen's ONE week once — on `screened` or `cut_down` the screen leaf, at the head of the next transit pass and before the arrival check (its seal read first), asks THE ONE HOLD HELPER `holdBeforeNode` for its FREEZE mode — the unit held EXACTLY where the prior tick left it, one week, no refund (RULINGS-ROUND3 A.2; PS §4.1 owns the helper, its two modes and their arithmetic; PR keeps none of its own) — never the border clamp, which serves only a node the unit passed this tick (a screen's contact is mid-hop, where the clamp would move the van FORWARD); on `withdrew` nothing is held [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PS-r2-15 (PS-r2-verify's fail (a)(b)): the r2b "the transit record's two clocks shift one week … (`departTick + 1`, `arrivalTick + 1`, `position01` restored), applied by the screen leaf" was a registrant's own arithmetic beside PS9's one helper, against PS9's "never by a registrant's own arithmetic"]. At a ONE-WEEK advance the card is docketed and the hold lands at the next advance; at a MONTH, SEASON or YEAR advance the default resolves in its own tick and holds the same one week — never two at either length [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-19: as compiled, a docketed screen could hold the van for its hearing week and then add `screened`'s week]. Where THE PASSAGE is lit, the week counts INTO the march's ONE cumulative hold cap (`PASSAGE_HOLD_CAP_WEEKS = SIEGE_MAX_AGE − MAX_MARCH_WEEKS − 1`, DERIVED; PS9's Bound 2 — every hold of one march, passage, fort or screen, shares it: RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 4); where THE PASSAGE is dark no `marches` row exists and the helper bounds the screen's hold by Bound 1 alone, the termination law (RULINGS-ROUND3 A.2: "PS9's Bound 2 … binds ONLY where THE PASSAGE is lit") — so the termination bound holds either way [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PS-r2-15 (c)]; the unit's KEY is its deployment id, stable across the held week (RULINGS-ROUND2b PS-r2-3 — never `departTick`), so PS's crossing rows and the screen's `causedBy` keep one key [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-21]. The screen's CARD is PR's wherever it is fought — on the approach to the riders' own town, or on its land as an enemy crosses toward a third town while PS's `armed_incursion` hears the holder's answer and keeps the home-defence battle (RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 1: "PR hosts the SCREEN card (PS1); PS keeps the home-defence battle"). **Host:** PS's THE TRANSIT LEAF STAGE (`transitLeafStage.js :: TRANSIT_LEAVES`, a closed registration list; PS §4.1) gains `patrolScreenLeaf` in THE ONE ORDER OF REGISTRANTS, written once in PS §4.1 and cited here, never restated with arithmetic: PS's border leaf → THE SITES' approach-fort leaf → THE PATROLS' screen leaf → WC Arc A's `armiesMeetLeaf` (one hold per record per tick, the first to hold wins) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PS-r2-15 (a): the r2b "after PS's border leaf and WC Arc A's `armies_meet` leaf" gave the one closed, pinned list a second order] — it inherits PS's car-0 and its STOP (S-a): if the transit kernel cannot be relieved, NO screen exists (the notice still does). The contest is `patrol_engages` (§11.2) — `screened` (held them a week), `cut_down` (held them a week and died doing it), `withdrew` (rode home with the word; no delay). A real army brushes a patrol aside: the odds of `cut_down` rise with the army's band over the detachment's (DRAFT, one keyed draw); a held `fortification` or `crossing` site on the stretch (ST) shifts the default toward `screened` (an anchor to hold). The levy's week is the town's to use (ST item 17: the ready keep is the first line of defence; the riders buy the week on the roads beside it, never as its garrison — A.1; WC-5's call-ins go out) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.1: "patrols are the keep's road arm" was the chair's withdrawn L-PR item 3]. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — R-11: PS1 makes the DESTINATION's land never a passage, so an army marching on its target meets no PS card in the target's land; the target's riders' screen is therefore PR's own hearing. An enemy crossing a THIRD town's land is PS's `armed_incursion`; the riders' screen of it is PR's own card beside PS's (open question 1, RULED in round 2b).]
- **(c) SHELTER AND WARN.** Riders TURN BACK caravans on the threatened stretches (the stretch's believed watch reads hostile for the town's own movers — §8.3's avoid), ESCORT refugee columns away from the front (§10.4; POP's columns), and WARN the villages (the report's news line; the flight read follows the danger ledger by construction once the enemy feeds it). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 2: PS's enemy-column presence feeder is WIDENED to the destination's own land (one feeder, PS's commit), so the target's danger — and the landed flight — rise as the host approaches; PR adds nothing (PLAUSIBLE until PS's disposition writes it).]
- **(d) THE BORDER FORTS.** A held fort is already garrisoned — by the SITE's own complement, paid through its holder by the site's funding band, never by the holder's riders (ST10; RULINGS-ROUND3 A.1); in week one a fort on a stretch of the round anchors the screen (above). PR adds no garrison and draws none.
- **(e) THE ARMY'S ROAD, THE REAR, AND WHAT IS KEPT.** When the levy is up and the army marches, the court keeps its campaign count by default — never below its own peace count, one more wherever `max` allows (§3.2; PR-r2-3) — and those riders take the army's road out first (§3.2); the riders not kept fold into the field force, and the halves the kept riders cannot reach — the back roads, the rear — go unpatrolled: the slow half fades over four weeks, the counter term turns negative (§6.2), and brigandage rises in the rear and dens grow behind the lines (ST's dens and THE BODIES' gangs, by construction — BD §9.8 (a): "WARTIME IS THE GANGS' SEASON — the levy takes the riders (PR's fielded count falls, so security falls and habitat rises)") [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (6)] — except on the stretches a held, funded keep watches with its own garrison, whose rows stay (A.1). A chooser that KEEPS MORE riders keeps its rear safe and marches a smaller army — the draw taken at that deploy (`patrolDraw`, §6.6): ONE bounded chooser signal through THE CHOOSER'S SHARED-SIGNAL CAR (SX-08; the first chain to land owns the car; PR adds one term row, `keepRiders`, read from the court's risk posture — `strategicPosture.js`, requirement 10 — and the believed rear danger), read when the army is raised and not again until it is raised anew [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-3 · PR-r2-1].
- **(f) COUNTERFORCES.** A deceiver's false report mobilises for nothing (§7.4); standing readiness costs wages (§7.1); a cut-down patrol is a loss — the deed, the tale and a season without riders (§7.2; no grievance input of its own) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — "a loss the commons feel" read as the grievance input §7.2 struck in round 2b]; a menace's approach is met by a report and a withdrawal unless the riders group to its band (§5.3).

### 9.2 Checking it out (item 14)
- **(a) TRIGGERS — closed lists.** An APPROPRIATE STRESSOR in the patrol's territory: `INVESTIGABLE_STRESSOR_TYPES = monster_raider_pressure · criminal_corridor · insurgency · slave_revolt · infiltration` — each a LANDED stressor type key (measured against `STRESSOR_COUNTERFORCES`' and `STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES`' keys: `siege famine occupation political_fracture indebtedness betrayal infiltration disease_outbreak succession_void monster_raider_pressure insurgency religious_conversion_fracture slave_revolt rebellion coup_detat wartime mass_migration market_shock criminal_corridor magical_instability magic_deadzone`); never famine, plague, a market shock or the arcane crises as such. [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's "the raider row, crime, unrest and riots, a monster, a disappearance" mapped onto the landed keys: raiders and monsters are ONE type (`monster_raider_pressure`), crime is `criminal_corridor`, armed unrest is `insurgency`/`slave_revolt`; riots are the commons' ladder (`commonsVoiceKernel.js` `RUNG_RIOT`) inside the walls — the town watch's business, not the riders'; a disappearance has no stressor type and is an AUTHORED event (below).] An APPROPRIATE AUTHORED EVENT: an Edit Mode / event-composer event (`components/settlement/eventComposer/buildEvent.js`) whose type is on `INVESTIGABLE_EVENT_TYPES` (DRAFT: the composer's harm and disorder events — the institution events PT-r1-2 maps, a raid, a disappearance — named at car-0 from the composer's catalog) — investigable BY DEFAULT, the DM switches the look off per event (a boolean on the authored event's own payload, `noLook: true`, the edits-delta idiom); a PARTY impact leaves traces a patrol may find (PT's deed at the place, §9.3's "the party's traces").
- **(b) THE LOOK.** ONE patrol leaves its round for ONE week (`looking`; the round's lowest-priority stretch loses its rider — the cost) and goes to the place (the light-unit physics; a look farther than one hop is a converging week first). It reads what is there BY THE WORLD'S OWN BELIEF RULES: a site's holder (ST; a site under the court veil — `keptSecret`, ST §2.4 — is found only with a keyed draw, `LOOK_FIND_SECRET` 0.3), a menace's tracks (MN), a body (THE BODIES), the party's traces (PT's deed at the place in the last season, if its `proof` is `shown` or `spoken` — PT §11's visibility), or NOTHING. Seeded (stream `patrol:look:<settlementId>:<placeKey>:<tick>`). A FINDING IS A SIGHTING, written ONCE at the tick of seeing into the ONE belief of that holder's might — a row `{ id, mightBand, tick }` (the band seen then) INTO the looking court's own belief record of the holder, by that record's owner's door: MN's `believedMenaces` for a MENACE holder, BD's `believedBodyOf` for a BODY (RULINGS-ROUND5 A.5: "A sighting row `{ id, mightBand, tick }` is written INTO the sighting court's belief record … never onto ST's holder object"; ST as cured in round 5 strikes THE PATROLS' sighting input from its writer's closed list — "a patrol's look writes into the holder's own belief record by its owner's door (MN's, THE BODIES'), never through ST's writer") [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — A.5, PR's echo: the r4 write went through ST's writer onto the site's `seenBy`, which ST has withdrawn] (RULINGS-ROUND4 A.17: "a patrol's look" refreshes it; "never a live truth read, never a second belief") — and a kept-secret den found by a look is the at-hand court's WITNESS (A.16); the veil itself is lifted only by ST's `made_known`, never by the riders; PR keeps no belief of its own [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.16 · A.17 (the PR half: the look as a sighting producer) · BD's route (6): the r3 `hidden` was ST's withdrawn word (ST §2.4: "the compile's `hidden`/`revealed` WITHDRAWN (the map's PARTY secrecy states)") and is now also THE BODIES' `hiddenWays` homonym, so the constant is renamed `LOOK_FIND_SECRET` (FREE, `tokens-r4.out`)].
- **(c) THE REPORT.** A `patrol_report` into the town's belief (§9.3); a CALL-BOARD entry where a need is found (CH §14's `callBoard` read gains the report as a source row in CH's commit — the den in the hills is a `wildland` call); and a ZERO-CLICK SUGGESTION to the DM ("Kell's riders report a den at the old fort — mark it?") riding ST's and PT's doors (CHAIR-NOTES 3's batched, non-blocking suggestions) — never a decision made for the DM: the engine never places a site (ST3) and never writes a party deed.
- **(d) THE ACT, AT THEIR SIZE.** A small thing is handled on the spot (a band below the patrol's own band: §5.3's contest that week); a large thing is grouped against or withdrawn from with the report (§5.2).
- **(e) COUNTERFORCES.** A patrol away is a road unpatrolled (the round's cost); a planted false lead (IN) wastes it; a wrong look can be an ambush (a body stronger than the patrol at the place: `patrol_engages`, `cut_down` possible); a town with few riders investigates little and learns late.
- **(f) A QUARANTINE THE HOST SETS** (CHAIR-NOTES 11; plague-R7): when PG's quarantine verb exists (the host's own verb with a stance input), the riders ENFORCE it — a stance `closed` for people at a gate makes the town's riders on that gate's stretch turn travellers back (§8.2's stop at the stance) — never stand in for it; until the verb lands, patrols hold no quarantine line (the host document: "a patrol is never drawn … holding a quarantine line").

### 9.3 The report and the deed
`patrol_report` is a RECEIPT kind (no hearing — it decides nothing between two parties) and a rumor FEED ENTRY: assembled in `migrationRumors.js :: rumorCarrierParams` beside the `migration_flight` lane (+≈6 lines through a leaf `patrols/patrolReports.js`; host 83), ingested by the ONE writer `advanceRumorLedgers` with `partyIds` = [the patrol's town] (its hop-0 witness — `RumorContent.partyIds` means the involved SETTLEMENTS, RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH §5) plus every ALLIED town whose riders walked the same stretch that week (§10.1(b)'s shared eyes — a hop sooner, no new carrier), magnitude by the threat's band (MN's might↔magnitude map), never the exact count. Its content is typed (§2.7): `finding ∈ threat_seen · crossing_seen · riders_seen · found_nothing · catch · clash` — `riders_seen` (a neighbour's riders seen on the neighbour's half at a shared gate) is the producer the planners' `believedWatch` reads (§8.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-16]. Under infoMode `full` it travels and weathers as every telling does; under the news-only presets it is a news line; under the omniscient arm the town already knows (the report is the chronicle line only). **Deeds** (RN's door, never PR's writer): a catch of a named traveller, a cut-down patrol, a den cleared, a screen held — each a candidate `DEED_SOURCES` row PR names for RN's ENGINE-SOURCE slot — ONE ACT, ONE DEED, the hearing's owner minting (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.5): `patrol_clash` — an UNLED patrol's clash, DOER THE RIDERS' TOWN (`{ kind: 'settlement', id }`, a TALE: a court's deed moves no court's fame, for no court has fame — RN1, RULINGS-ROUND3 A.4; B.5: "an UNLED patrol clash mints `patrol_clash`'s own deed with the TOWN as doer"); a patrol CUT DOWN by a NAMED body, DOER THE BODY with its leading paragon as `ledBy` (§7.2); a clash INSIDE A LED HUNT mints NONE — the mission's close is the hunt's one door (CH's `hunt_won`, doer the leader; the body's `escaped` where it fails — §5.5); `patrol_catch` — a catch of a named traveller, doer the riders' town, or the named paragon riding with them that week (§10.5; RN's row) — each minted by THE ONE PERSON STAGE from the verdict record (RN's rule, B of RULINGS-ROUND2); until RN opens the slot, the receipt is the record [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.5 · SEAMS-r4-7 (one success, two doors: RN's `patrol_clash` with the leader as doer and CH's `hunt_won`) · the RN→PR route: the r4 sentence made "the paragon who led the riders" `patrol_clash`'s doer, the second door].

---

## §10 ALLIES, THE KINGDOM AND THE PASSAGE (the owner's second word; items 7, 8, 12)

### 10.1 Allied and kingdom patrols meeting (item 12)
- **(a) RECOGNITION IS BELIEF.** Two powers' riders on one stretch know each other as far as their towns' beliefs go: each reads `belief(own, other).allianceLabel` (truth when beliefs are dark). Allies and members of one polity (PL's walk, PL1) pass without contest — members while the rung between them stands; across a BROKEN rung (a feud, a break, a contest that suspends the link) recognition is lost and the pair stance decides (§10.3 (g)) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7 (F.5)]. A STALE belief — an alliance broken, a war begun this week and not yet heard — makes an incident between friends: one side believes `hostile`, the other `allied`, and the meeting is a `patrol_engages` card whose default is `withdrew` for the side that believes the other a friend (DRAFT; the stale side does not strike first unless martial). A provocateur (CH) stages one with a false report (§7.4).
- **(b) SHARED EYES.** Allied riders — and the riders of members under one unbroken banner — on a shared stretch EXCHANGE what they saw: each town becomes a hop-0 witness of the other's `patrol_report` from that stretch that week (§9.3) — a belief channel a hop sooner, never a new carrier; a dense allied net sees its whole land. Feuding members share no eyes (RULINGS-ROUND3 F.6) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7].
- **(c) JURISDICTION = PS's LADDER.** A patrol stepping onto a half BEYOND its own territory (past the digest gate — a round never does, §2.2; only a pursuit or an escort) is a military unit crossing and PS applies (`passageStandingOf`, PS4; PS's `PASSAGE_MOVER_KINDS` holds `patrol`, PS §2.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-8], with ONE lean: `passageSizeBand` of a lone patrol is `quiet` against almost any holder and PS's tolerance width for a `quiet` unit is spent at half a week per week (PS §4.4) — so an ally's patrol rides freely for weeks on end (a `fair` width lasts eight weeks for a `quiet` unit, a `wide` one a season; an ally's width RISES while it is itself at war — RULINGS-ROUND2b PS-r2-1). An ally in HOT PURSUIT (a detachment with aim `pursue` following a body across the border) is `welcomed` as urgency at an ally's gate (receipted); a stranger's patrol must ask like any unit (PS's `passage_at_the_border`), and a cool ally's uninvited patrol reads PS's own `tolerated` or `refused_with_warning`; an ENEMY's patrol (the rung `enemy`) is never `refused` — no request is possible — its placement on the far half opens PS's `armed_incursion` card, PS fights NO border battle for it (a patrol has no transit record), `barred` refuses the placement that week (PR re-places it), `unopposed` lets it ride under the standing card, and where the holder's riders stand on that half the meeting of riders with riders is PR's own `patrol_engages` (a believed `hostile`, (a), (f)) (PS §5.5 as cured in round 5, its minor (g)) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PS's round-5 route to PR (`cure/D-PS-r5.md` §5), applied in this round so none is left unapplied]. INSIDE A KINGDOM the line is THE BANNER's (§10.3 (d)): where the crown's own hold reads STRONG and the rung between the two members stands, a member's patrol rides a fellow member's roads BY RIGHT where PS's jurisdiction read answers `right` (`passageStandingOf(…).banner`: the crown's hold as the HOLDER believes it is `staunch` or `unbroken` — PS §5.5, OD-PS-45) — no tolerance spent; PS opens a `banner` permit and ONE card defaulting `granted` at the first placement; where it is WEAK, the fellow member's riders cross as an ally's on this paragraph's ladder, with the `quiet` unit's half-week spend; across a broken rung the believed label decides as for any unit. The charter grants tolls, not passage (PL §4.5). [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — R-9: the brief's "members' patrols on each other's roads only by the polity-wide passage charter" — PL §4.5 cured SPL-M13 to "the CHARTER's passage is tolls only" and PS4 already makes members allies on the ladder; under a FRAYED hold members still ask, because a frayed member's believed label may not read ally.] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7 · SEAMS-r3-6 (RULINGS-ROUND3 F.3: "a STRONG hold makes the kingdom one net … a WEAK hold leaves each lord his own roads and treats a neighbour-lord's riders with PR item 12's ally size tolerance"): the r3 text spent the ally tolerance at every hold.]
- **(d) GROUPING ACROSS TOWNS — `patrol_joins` (CLIMAX).** Against a believed threat bigger than either town's riders can reach alone, a town may ASK an ally whose land or round touches the threat's place to JOIN its detachment: a decided act of the ally's court, verdicts `rides · will_see · refused` — `rides` (its riders join under their own command, §2.5), `will_see` (it sends none this week but turns its own round toward the shared stretch — its coverage there rises; a lesser help, no detachment), `refused`. The default reads the LANDED RELATIONSHIP PAIR STATE between the ally and the asker in EVERY arm — label, trust and resentment (`relationshipState`, law E14; RN's refused pairs leave court↔court feeling to it) — `allied` with high trust rides, cool trust will see, resentment or a non-allied label refuses — and its own believed danger; RN's regard enters only through a PERSON decider (the ally's acting officer) if a later wave wants one [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-11: an ally court's regard of the asking TOWN is a quantity RN never holds ("A holder `s:`/`c:` never holds an entry about a subject `s:`/`c:`"), and "warm · cold · hostile" are not RN's bands]. Credit: each town's riders are credited separately for a den cleared or a body driven off (WC-1's contribution idiom → regard through RN's door when lit; a court-to-court `contributionLedger` row when WC-1 lands); a refusal is remembered on the relationship record (the one relationship writer, a small resentment patch — DRAFT).
- **(e) THE HOME HAS FIRST CALL** on its own roads (RULINGS-ROUND3 F.1: "the home has FIRST CALL and takes charge on its own roads"): the crown's riders and the net's lent riders fill only the halves a home leaves unwatched, never displacing its own (§10.3 (d)) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7: the r3 "THE CROWN'S RIDERS HAVE PRECEDENCE where the hold is `staunch` or `unbroken`", under which "members' riders defer", is reversed by F.1].
- **(f) FRICTION.** Coverage CAPS PER ROAD: `watchOn` is the MAX over the powers on a road, never the sum (overlap is waste) — the danger read; `watchOf` keeps each power's own for the encounter (§8.1). Members in a FEUD (PL §6) treat each other's riders as hostile on shared stretches: a meeting is `patrol_engages` (two settlements' units, requirement 24 in its plainest form), and a clash is the feud escalating — the liege's arbitration (`polity_arbitration`, PL §6) is the road out, never PR's. A den on a shared border is ONE contest (RULINGS-ROUND4 A.18, ST's rule): the ANCHOR court's detachment (the site's attachment) contests first; a second at-hand court's detachment that rides JOINS it as one force — the bands summed, the anchor named the clearer, the joiner receipted and credited (§10.1 (d)'s idiom) — and contests alone only where the anchor does not ride; never two contests [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.18: the r3 "cleared by whoever believes and acts first (ST's claim rule, ST13); credit is shared only if grouped" read two at-hand courts as racing].
- **(g) ESCORT HANDOVER AT THE GATE.** An escort (§10.4) rides to its town's border gate; the next territory's riders take the caravan if they stand on the gate's stretch that week and their town is not hostile to it, else it proceeds unescorted; the crown's riders escort end to end inside the kingdom.

### 10.2 Who asks whom
`patrol_joins` is asked only by a town whose own fielded riders cannot reach the threat's band (§5.2) — a hunt's included (§5.5) — only of allies or members, and at most one ask per threat per week (DRAFT; the next ask waits for the threat to grow) — so the docket carries a join only when one is needed. Under a strong banner a fellow member's riders who ride cross by right (§10.3 (d)); the JOIN itself stays that member's decision on this card — the banner spares the crossing, never the choice [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7].

### 10.3 THE BANNER — every patrol in a kingdom (the owner's ~19:2x word, item 9 above: "every patrol in a kingdom is working under the highest overlord banner, and every subsequent level until it reaches them inteh vassal chain with a home local font thet is their home settlement"; RULINGS-ROUND3 §F.1–F.7; items 7, 12(e))
[CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RULINGS-ROUND4 A.7 · SEAMS-r3-6 · the PL→PR route (`cure/D-PL-r3.md` §5, PR-r3-verify ROUTED-UNAPPLIED): this section is REWRITTEN as THE BANNER. The r3 section read: crown's riders "only where the crown's OWN read of its hold is `staunch` or `unbroken` … AND its reach into the member is live, `crownReachAt(…).share01 > 0` (… nothing under a frayed link); under `shaky` they keep to the root's own land and the roads between members; under `frayed` they keep to the root's land and members ignore them", and "members' riders defer". F.3 makes the WEAK hold the ally-tolerance arm (not "members ignore them"), F.1 gives the HOME first call, and the `share01 > 0` clause is struck (under the crown's own `staunch`+ read PL5's reach into a sub-vassal is at least 0.02 — PL §15.12 — so it shut nothing out).]
- **(a) ONE HOME, A STACK OF BANNERS (F.1).** Every patrol has ONE home — the settlement that raises, pays and houses it: its envelope is the home's (§3), its round the home's (§2.2), and on the home's OWN halves the home has FIRST CALL (its riders are placed first; nobody's riders displace them, §10.1 (e)). Its LOYALTY STACK is PL's BANNER STACK — "NO NEW READ: a patrol's stack is `polityOf(worldState, snapshot, homeId).path` (home → … → paramount) read rung by rung with `crownReachAt` at each rung, and CUT at the first rung whose link `swornLinkSuspended` reads true" (PL §2, §4.7 F.1) — DERIVED at every read and NEVER STORED: when the chain changes (a fealty sworn, a break, a sale, a conquest, a contest) the banners change at the next read, and no patrol row holds a loyalty; PR mints no stack read of its own. A town in no polity has a stack of one, its own banner. On scrutiny a patrol's card shows its home and its stack as the chain ("Kell's riders — sworn to Tolle, under the crown of Merewick"). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7, aligned with PL's round-4 §4.7 THE BANNER (read 22:0x; `cure/D-PL-r4.md` §5's route to PR) — PL owns the reads and the rows, PR the composition and the cap]
- **(b) THE CALL — NEVER SKIPPING A LORD'S ROW (F.1, as RE-RULED).** A lord's call on riders is PL's SUMMONS — the riders an input line on `call_in_answer` (OD-PL-50; (e) below: `answered` · `half_answered` keeps them on the member's roads · `refused`), a hearing with its fealty row. Under EVERY hold below `unbroken` the OVERLORD's call reaches a patrol rung by rung: the crown calls its direct vassals; each answers for its own riders and calls its own (PL §4.1: "the crown summons its direct vassals; each mid-lord answers for itself and summons its own"). Under `unbroken` — the crown's own read, `polityHoldOf(…, crownId, crownId)` (PL §4.1) — the crown MAY summon any member DIRECTLY: the riders' line goes to that member on that member's own `call_in_answer`, and EVERY LORD BETWEEN still has its ROW (PL §4.1 (ii), F.1: "every lord between still has its row — its credit when it lets the men go, the precedence friction when the crown rode past it"): where the lord itself answered the same call `answered` or `half_answered`, it LET its vassal's men go — the lender's yearly credit (PL F.6 (a): one `service_answered` row a year, episode `riders_lent:<rootId>:<yearStartTick>`, no patch); where the crown did not summon it in that call, or it answered `refused` or let the call lapse, the crown RODE PAST IT — the precedence friction, ONE `riders_quartered` row carrying ONE resentment patch (PL F.6 (b) (ii); §7.6), episode the call-in request's key; nothing is written for a lord between when the member refused. Every summons row sits on the answering member's OWN sworn edge, to its immediate liege, whoever called (PL §4.1 (i): "the vassal of my vassal is not my vassal"). PR mints none of these: they are PL's, read from the call's own answers [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — STOP-PR-r4-1, RE-RULED (RULINGS-ROUND4 §E: "PL §4.1 STANDS: under `unbroken` the crown may summon every member DIRECTLY … §F.1's 'never skipping a lord' is AMENDED to 'never skipping a lord's ROW' … PR §10.3 (b) carries both arms") · SEAMS-r4-3 · RULINGS-ROUND5 A.8 (PL's half, read at PL §4.1 (i)–(ii) and F.1 as cured 04:00): the r4 passage was written to the one-arm F.1 and marked `[⛔ STOP-PR-r4-1]`; the STOP is closed].
- **(c) ONE SHARE LAW, AND A LEND THAT NEVER READS ZERO AT A STRONG HOLD (F.2; RULINGS-ROUND5 B.8).** The liege's share of a member's RIDERS is the SAME share as its voice in every decision — PL's row (PL §4.7 F.2 as cured in round 5: "PL4's `POLITY_LIEGE_SHARE[band]` of the member's seat weight toward its IMMEDIATE liege, at every tier — never a second share for riders"; `marginal 0.10 · present 0.25 · strong 0.38 · dominant 0.45`), read at the FIRST rung of the member's BANNER STACK ((a); PL §2) — PR keeps no share of its own. Under the crown's own read `staunch` or `unbroken` ((d)) each member m LENDS up to `lend(m) = max(1, ⌊n(m) × s(m) × hold01⌋)` of its `n(m)` riders on the round — `s(m)` its band share toward its immediate liege, `hold01` the crown's own read (PL §3.1 (e)) — but only while it keeps at least THE HOME'S MINIMUM itself, `n(m) − lend(m) ≥ NET_HOME_MIN` (DRAFT 1, UNMEASURED — PR's constant beside the per-stretch cap: one rider always stays on the round's first half, §5.2's rule), else it lends none (a one-rider hamlet keeps its rider); a SUB-VASSAL lends through its LIEGE's line, up the chain rung by rung (F.1): its riders join the line its liege's banner carries, counted ONCE — never re-scaled at each rung and never computed at the crown's own share (PL F.2: "`crownReachAt`'s paramount reach is the crown's voice in a sub-vassal's DECISIONS, PL5, not a call on its men"). `lend(m)` is a CEILING: the net takes from m's round, in REVERSE priority, only the riders it PLACES on a need ((d)); the rest stay on the home's round. The crown's own riders join at `min(⌊n(root) × hold01⌋, n(root) − NET_HOME_MIN)` under `command: 'paramount'` ("the crown's riders" in prose, R-8), so a crown keeps first call on its own roads too [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — the home's minimum applied to the root as to every member: at `unbroken`'s `hold01` 1.0 the r4 floor let a crown lend every rider it had]. The net is the Σ of the riders PLACED (each member's ≤ `lend(m)`) plus the root's, the per-stretch cap binding (PL F.2: "PL owns the share, the floor rule and the hold; PR the composition, the minimum and the cap"). EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r5-probe/net-r5.out` (every tier × band × the hold's four ends, at the peace count and at `max`): no member with two or more riders lends 0 at a strong hold (0 rows; under r4 a town lent 0 below `strong` and every sub-vassal 0), no lender keeps fewer than the minimum (0 rows), R39's Ashford (a town, three riders on the round, any band toward Kell) lends 1 at hold 0.60 (r4: 0), ten town vassals at `strong` lend 10 (r4: 0; PL's r4 Σ-then-floor 9). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7, aligned with PL's round-4 §4.7 THE BANNER (read 22:0x; `cure/D-PL-r4.md` §5's route to PR) — PL owns the reads and the rows, PR the composition and the cap — the round's first draft read the share AT the member and named the crown's own share `POLITY_LIEGE_SHARE.dominant`; PL's row is the crown's own read and `share01` 1 × `hold01` for the root] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X1 (MAJOR, EXECUTED by the verifier, `skeptic/PR-r4-verify-probe/net.out`: "At the crown's `staunch` read … no sub-vassal of any tier ever lends, and a town-sized direct vassal lends 1 only at band `strong`+") · RULINGS-ROUND5 B.8 ("a member lends `max(1, floor(n × share01 × hold01))` riders when it keeps at least the minimum (DRAFT) itself; SUB-VASSALS lend through their LIEGE's line … never computed at the crown's own share") · the PL→PR route (`cure/D-PL-r5.md` §5: "§10.3 (c) the lend per B.8 at the member's own band share toward its immediate liege (PL F.2)")]
- **(d) THE LINE BLURS BY THE HOLD (F.3).** Each decision of the banner has ONE owner and ONE read, never two reads of one fact. (1) WHETHER A CROSSING IS BY RIGHT is THE PASSAGE's jurisdiction read (F.3: "PS's jurisdiction read for same-kingdom riders reads the hold"): `passageStandingOf(worldState, snapshot, holderId, riderHomeId, unit).banner` — `right` where the rider's home and the half's holder are members of ONE polity with no believed `hostile` between them and the crown's hold AS THE HOLDER BELIEVES IT (`polityHoldOf(…, rootId, holderId).band`) is `staunch` or `unbroken`, `tolerance` where it is `frayed` or `shaky`, nothing across a broken rung (PS §5.5, OD-PS-45 — PS3's law, "B answers on its own belief"; re-read at every placement); PR keeps NO read of its own for it. (2) WHETHER THE CROWN CALLS THE NET AT ALL — composes its riders and its members' lent riders across the kingdom — is the crown's decision on its OWN read (`polityHoldOf(…, rootId, rootId)` `staunch` or `unbroken`, PL §3.1 (e), the site that already decides the crown's riders); and HOW MANY of a member's riders it may lend is PL's one share times the crown's own `hold01` ((c)); the receiving member's right is its own belief (PL §3.1 (f), PS's read). Each decision reads its own decider's belief, PL §3.1's rule, and the right and the net never disagree: the net places no lent rider where the right is absent [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7, aligned with PS's round-4 cure read at 22:0x (PS §5.5, P33, OD-PS-45): PR's first draft of this round read the crossing's right off the crown's own read, which would have let a crown's confidence override what a doubting member sees — PS's vetoable alternative, not its ruling]. Then:
  - **STRONG — THE KINGDOM IS ONE NET.** With the net called, a member's patrol rides a fellow member's roads BY RIGHT OF THE BANNER wherever that member answers `right` — a pursuit, an escort, a hunt, a join, a lent rider — with no tolerance spent: PS opens a `banner` permit and ONE `passage_at_the_border` card defaulting `granted` at the first placement on that member's land, no hold (PS's; the DM may still tilt or decide it, requirement 24). Coverage COMPOSES across members' borders on the shared stretches: after every home has placed its own round (§4.1 step 5), THE NET places the crown's riders on any member's halves and each member's lent riders (up to its `lend(m)`, (c)) on the fellow member's half of a road SHARED with its home's land — only on a half left unwatched this week (`never`), only where that half's holder answers `right` (a lent rider is never sent to spend an ally's width), and only where a NEED stands on it, in this order: (i) the member's OWN riders went to the levy (F.4: protection, (f)); (ii) a believed threat on it that the member's own riders cannot reach (§5.1–5.2); (iii) the crown's war roads and trade (the crown's own priority, §2.2's terms read for the root); (iv) in PEACE, only where the crown's own peace-posture fork keeps more riders out (`peaceShare(root) > PEACE_SHARE`, §3.2) — the crown's call on the net in peace, whose price is (h). The crown's OWN riders may still ride a half whose holder answers `tolerance` — as an ally's patrol on its width (the weak arm below, from that holder's belief). The net's rows are the lending power's half rows keyed by that holder, `weekly` while their need stands and dropped when it ends (a first-response change, §2.3); the per-stretch cap stops stacking (`watchOn` is the MAX, §10.1 (f)); the net never displaces a home's own rider. A net rider on a member's half WITNESSES for it (PR10) and counts on a known trail's junction (§2.6 `trailWatchOf`); the GATE's catch stays the holder's own riders' (J385, §6.4).
  - **WEAK — EACH LORD HIS OWN ROADS** (the crown's own read `shaky`/`frayed`). The crown composes NO net and quarters no riders: "its own riders keep to the root's land" (PL §4.7 F.3, §15.12); members' rounds stay their own; and any rider that does cross a member's border — a pursuit, an escort, a hunt, the crown's own included — crosses on PS's ladder as an ALLY's patrol (members stand as allies, PS4) with PR's ally size tolerance (a `quiet` unit spending at half a week per week, §10.1 (c)). The same holds at a single border whose holder believes the hold weak under a strong crown (PS's `tolerance`): no lent rider is placed there. `frayed` no longer means "members ignore the crown's riders": it is this arm. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7, aligned with PL's round-4 §4.7 THE BANNER (read 22:0x; `cure/D-PL-r4.md` §5's route to PR) — PL owns the reads and the rows, PR the composition and the cap]
  - **BROKEN** — recognition lost; see (g).
- **(e) THE CROWN'S CALL ON THE RIDERS AT WAR — PL's summons; no kind of PR's** (F.1's call, rung by rung, (b)). At war, the crown's call on a member's fighting men is PL-5's SUMMONS, answered on WC's `call_in_answer` (`answered · half_answered · refused · lapsed`; PL §4.1), and a member's riders are inside that levy (PR3) — so the riders ride the SAME card as ONE input line: `answered` — the member's riders go with its levy (those the crown's round can use ride the war's roads under `command: 'paramount'` for the war's season, credited like WC-1's contribution); `half_answered` — the member sends its levy and KEEPS its riders on its own roads (PL §4: "Half service — WC's `half_answered`; a `service_answered` row with the fraction"); `refused` — neither (PL prices it `service_refused` through its own writer, as for any refusal); `lapsed` — undecided. PR mints NO summons kind, no second vocabulary and no fealty row of its own, and PR itself requests no fealty row; the `patrol:` prefix PL registers in `FEALTY_EPISODE_PREFIXES` (RULINGS-ROUND3 B, PL-V4: "riders and a retained company are units; harbouring them is priced") is PS's request, not PR's — a patrol as a passage MOVER (`unitKey = 'patrol:' + detachmentId`, PS §2.1) whose crossing a member grants while it is at war with the member's liege (PL7's harbouring price, PS §7.7); the service rows the summons writes light under `polityObligationsEnabled` (RULINGS-ROUND3 A.5) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PL-V4 (PR's sentence "no `requestFealtyRow` prefix is needed for riders" read as denying the prefix PL now registers) · A.5] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-17 · SEAMS-r2-10: `patrol_order` double-heard the men PL's summons already hears, reversed `relief_answer`'s subject, and named the relationship writer's internal append instead of PL's request door; R-13 is withdrawn — the fold already uses `answered` for this act].
- **(f) THE CROWN'S PROTECTION IS FELT — AND RECORDED AS A DEED (F.4).** THE OCCASION is PL's: a member answers its liege's summons `answered` WITH ITS RIDERS (their line sent, (e)) while the crown's own read is `staunch`+ — the member's roads are then the net's to cover, need (i), first in the net's order; below `staunch` no occasion opens ("a crown that cannot hold the kingdom as one net promises no cover", PL §4.7 F.4). Whether the net actually RODE is a fact PR KEEPS and exports — THE BANNER'S SEASON LEDGER (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.9): the member's `weeksCovered` pair (§2.7), the weeks of the season in which a rider of any power OTHER than the member's own (the crown's, a fellow member's) stood placed on at least one of its road halves, counted by the stage from each week's `lived` rows (one placement, R30) and replaced at the season's turn (`seasonStartTick = tick − (tick mod INTERVAL_WEEKS.one_season)`, PL's boundary), read through `bannerCoverOf(worldState, memberId, tick) → { weeks, seasonStartTick }` (§2.6). PL writes ONE row on the member's OWN edge to its immediate liege, whoever's summons it answered (PL §4.1 (i)) — `protection_kept` when `weeks > 0`, `protection_failed` when 0 — at the SEASON'S END (its last tick, after PR's stage has counted that week: §4.1's clock) or at the member's riders' return home if that comes first (the tally so far); an occasion opened in a season's last month is judged on the NEXT season's tally (PL F.4's REFINED clause); never from the one-week `lived` rows [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X2 (MAJOR: "`lived` is ONE week … the net rides weeks 1–10, then the hold slips. At the season's end the read sees no rider and PL writes `protection_failed`") · B.9 · the PL→PR route (`cure/D-PL-r5.md` §5: "§2.6/§10.3 (f) declare `weeksCovered` with its reset at `tick − (tick mod INTERVAL_WEEKS.one_season)`"); EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r5-probe/season-r5.out`: the verifier's case reads `protection_failed` under r4 and 10 weeks → `protection_kept` under r5, a stale pair of another season reads 0] (PL's writer, PL's OBLIGATION family; "Came to its aid" is never a number: the PLACEMENT is the deed and the count never enters — PL-V5's principle). PR requests no fealty row and mints none. A BODY sees ONE banner: its believed coverage composes the kingdom's riders by what it has heard (§8.3: `believedWatch` is banner-blind for a body) — deterrence by belief, never by ownership (THE BODIES §5.3 cites it). [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7, aligned with PL's round-4 §4.7 THE BANNER (read 22:0x; `cure/D-PL-r4.md` §5's route to PR) — PL owns the reads and the rows, PR the composition and the cap]
- **(g) CIVIL WAR, FEUD AND BREAK — THE GAP CLOSED (F.5).** A patrol FOLLOWS ITS HOME: its home's lord decides the side, and the banners re-derive at the next read ((a): nothing stored to unwind). PL's three cases (PL §4.7 F.5): a BREAK re-points the breaker's link to `rival`, so the walk no longer reaches its old liege — its patrols follow their home and THE CROWN'S OWN RIDERS in its land are a FOREIGN UNIT: PS's passage rules apply to them as to any army, trespass by hostility included; a FEUD leaves every link sworn and the stack above unchanged, but the two members' patrols lose recognition by their pair's own war state (§10.1 (a), (f): a believed `hostile` makes a meeting `patrol_engages`) and share no eyes; a POLITY WAR keeps every link sworn in storage, but while `swornLinkSuspended` holds the STACK IS CUT at the suspended link — a patrol follows its home's side, and the crown's riders in a claimant-side member's land are a foreign unit. PR's net composition reads the stack THROUGH the predicate and joins PL §5.3's GUARDED set ("the walker reds a reader of the vassal type that does not"); lent riders across a cut or a feud go home. THE FIRST-VISIBLE SIGN (requirement 16): riders TURNED BACK at a border they rode freely last season — where the right has lapsed the next placement asks PS's ladder (§10.1 (c)), and a refusal is the receipt: "Merewick's riders were turned back at the Kell ford, where they rode freely last season." [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7, aligned with PL's round-4 §4.7 THE BANNER (read 22:0x; `cure/D-PL-r4.md` §5's route to PR) — PL owns the reads and the rows, PR the composition and the cap]
- **(h) COUNTERFORCES (F.6).** Riders lent to the net leave the home THINNER — they are taken from its round in reverse priority, so its lowest halves go unridden (§4.1 step 4's rule) — PR's half. PL's halves (PL §4.7 F.6): (a) THE LENDER'S CREDIT — a member whose riders the net placed on a fellow member's road halves in a year writes ONE `service_answered` row that year on its edge to its liege (episode `riders_lent:<rootId>:<yearStartTick>`; PL's row — a row a year, so the 24-row record keeps its oath rows); (b) THE SWEPT LORD'S GRUDGE — the PRECEDENCE FRICTION, ONE `riders_quartered` ROW on the lord's own sworn edge carrying ONE resentment patch, PL's own row and never weave wear: THE SWEEP read at the season's end from the season tally where no cover occasion stood, THE RIDE PAST once per call under `unbroken` ((b) above) (§7.6; PL §4.7 F.6 (b) as cured in round 5) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — RULINGS-ROUND5 A.8 · the PL→PR route]; (c) THE PEACETIME CALL — a lend in a season in which neither the root nor the lender is at war wears as `riders_lent`, one typed decaying incident per lender per season on its sworn edge (resentment, hence grip: "a crown that calls riders from every member in peace pays in resentment across the chain"), while in war the lend is service and wears nothing; (d) the per-stretch cap stops stacking, and feuding members share no eyes (§10.1 (b)). PR writes none of these: PL's writer reads PR's SEASON TALLY (`bannerCoverOf`, §2.6) and the call's own answers [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.9: never the one-week `lived` rows]. [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7, aligned with PL's round-4 §4.7 THE BANNER (read 22:0x; `cure/D-PL-r4.md` §5's route to PR) — PL owns the reads and the rows, PR the composition and the cap]
- **(i) THE SURFACE (F.7).** One felt sentence: "The crown's riders keep the Tolle hills since Kell's riders went to the war." [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — §F.7's words are "The crown's wardens ride the Tolle hills …": `warden` is CH's ARCHETYPE and the public-security OFFICE that L-BD 41 names — a person who LEADS riders and knows the ways (§5.5, §10.5) — and a landed role label besides (R-8); on one card beside "Ilsa, a warden, rides with Kell's watch" the word would name two things, so the prose keeps PR's "the crown's riders".] On scrutiny the card shows the home and the stack as the chain, the hold's band and the need that placed the rider ("Ashford's riders on Kell's half of the ford road, by the crown's banner: Kell's riders are with the levy"); the machinery (the share, the floor, the order of needs) is never pushed. The map draws the banner's colour when the host lights patrols: the window's row carries the stack's `rootId` (§13; H9/H23 — never H16).

### 10.4 The border hand and the escorts (items 4, 12(g); with PS)
- **PS's `granted_under_watch` escort IS a detachment of the holder's riders** (aim `escort`), drawn from the holder's fielded count for the weeks it walks — the riders the escort takes are off the holder's round (the cost). [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — PS §2.3 prices the escort as "subtracted from B's home defence for the weeks it walks"; with PR lit, the riders ARE the escort, so the subtraction is from the round, not the walls — PS's disposition aligns (a seam row; dark PR keeps PS's home-defence arm).]
- **CARAVAN AND COLUMN ESCORTS (WY, POP).** A funded court escorts its own caravans and its refugee columns through its own land when a believed threat stands on their route: `escortedOn(worldState, mover, roadKey, tick)` → the escort's band; WY-6's `caravan_seized` odds fall with it and POP's column road deaths fall with it (each consumer's commit; one read). Aggregate goods need no escort row: §6.3's presence lean is their escort.
- **A PATROL CROSSING A FOREIGN BORDER** is PS's unit (§10.1(c)); a `barred` answer's contest is PS's, and with PR lit the holder's blocking force against a `quiet` unit may be its riders — PS's card, PS's outcomes (a border hearing, not a screen; RULINGS-ROUND2b Q1 gives PR only the screen's card).

### 10.5 Champions and sites (item 8)
- **A WARDEN LEADS** (CH): a `wildland` paragon of champion polarity whose weekly choice places it with a town's riders (CH §4.4's weights gain a "ride with the watch" row in CH's commit) lifts the quality of the town's riders — ONE bounded MULTIPLIER, CH's `paragonLean(worldState, settlementId, 'wildland', 'patrol_rider_quality')` — the seam word of CH's closed `PARAGON_LEAN_SEAMS` with its declared deviation `d` 0.10 (CH §2.2: "`patrol_rider_quality` 0.10 (PR §10.5's warden riding with a town's riders, applied as `quality × mult`)"; "`→ { mult, reason, wnpcIds } | null`", read from the paragon's `exertion.outcome` only) — applied as `quality × mult`, a factor above one reading toward BETTER riders (an exerting champion's `1 + d`) and below one toward worse (a villain's `1 − d`), PR's own clamp the quality's [0, 1] bound, never a band step and never an additive shift, the receipt naming it ("Ilsa, a warden, rides with Kell's watch") [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.15 (`paragonLean` returns one form): the r2b "lifts their quality one step … ONE `{ mult, reason }` lean" mixed a band step with a multiplier] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — RULINGS-ROUND4 A.10 · SEAMS-r3-9 · the CH→PR route (`cure/D-CH-r3.md` §5; PR-r3-verify ROUTED-UNAPPLIED): the call named a `side` and "±0.05 / ±0.10" where CH's fourth parameter is the seam word and its `d` is 0.10]; where a person of the warden archetype HOLDS the town's public-security office (CH's `isWardenParagon`, or an office-holder's roster role `warden`), the town knows the hidden ways of its land (the office's knowledge in BD's one known set `knownTrailsOf`, §5.5; L-BD 41) — a warden merely RETAINED by the court and holding no office adds no way (RULINGS-ROUND5 B.13) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.13] — and a warden may LEAD a hunt (§5.5; L-BD 40); a MAGISTRATE's order (CH's `order` domain) raises the town's will gate one step for a season (a lean on §3.1's WILL, its reason named).
- **A VILLAIN'S NETWORK EVADES OR BUYS THE RIDERS:** its runners meet the one notice law (§8.1 — fame read at the riders' town is their only cover; no cover-identity term exists, PR-V4) and an unpaid watch's `BRIBE_KEEP` (§6.4) applies to them as to goods; exposure is the corruption web's (`applyForeignExposureBlowback`, `covert_hire_exposed`).
- **THE KEEP AND THE RIDERS — two bodies of men, one cap** (ST §7.4's row; RULINGS-ROUND3 A.1, which corrects the chair's L-PR item 3 "the fort's road-danger-down effect IS its patrols"): a held `fortification` puts its attached halves FIRST on its HOLDER's round (§2.2 — a refinement that stays), and the riders who walk them are the holder town's OWN, drawn from its one envelope (§3). The keep's GARRISON is the SITE's own complement — paid through the holder by the site's funding band, drawn from no town's manpower pool and never from its riders; it neither marches nor is drawn (ST §4.1). So the keep's road rows (ST §3.3: presence suppression 0.20, raider counterforce +0.15, the `monsterRaiderGate` ×0.8 · corridor ×0.85 · infiltration ×0.9 rows) STAND while the keep is held and funded, lit or dark, and the riders' rows are the riders' own: two DISTINCT feeders of ONE capped presence suppression (`min(0.25, σ_site + σ_patrol)`, MN §3.3) and ONE counterforce composition (ST's slot, one ±0.15 clamp) — different men, so no double count; the cap is the runaway guard. When the levy takes the riders, the riders' subtraction and deviation go with them (PR3, §9.1(e)) and the keep's rows stay: "the hill road beyond the keep's reach has gone to the brigands" (§18, sentence 9). PR mints no gate row (PR5) and keeps no keep number [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-r2-8 (FAILED in round 2b: "only the fort's 0.20 suppression yields while its watch gate rows and +0.15 outlive the levy, against R34") · PR-V2 (MN's M53 "0.20 and its riders 0.15" stands as pinned under the cap, MN citing ST's `σ`) · SEAMS-r2-9 (one body of men → two bodies, one cap): the r2b yielding and `fortWill` are STRUCK]. A held `watchtower` extends the riders' NOTICE (§8.1's `tower`) and witnesses for them (ST17); DENS are what riders clear (ST's `site_cleared`, §5.3). **SITES CONSIDERED** (RULINGS-ROUND2 E; ST §7.4's coverage row for PR, "BY BOTH"): the round reads the fort's attachment, the slow half shares ST's suppressor and cap, the clearing is ST's card, the tower is ST's witness — PR keeps no site number and mints no site record [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the one "sites considered" line].

## §11 THE DM'S HAND AND THE HEARINGS

### 11.1 `PATROL_ORDER` — one realm verb (≤ 4 dials; a realm-manifest row; approval-routed; the edits-delta overlay; premium; the AI proposes it typed)
| Dial | Values | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `settlement` | target: a settlement (the proposal's anchor `targetSaveId`; the actor convention) | whose riders |
| `place` | target: a road half — the stretch key `stretch:<a\|b>` cut at the digest gate, the holder the order's settlement (§2.2) — a site or a BODY (`subject` dial; a body `{ kind: 'body', id }`, RULINGS-ROUND4 A.12), or `round` | where the order points [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-17: "a gate" bare is not a place] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.14: the landed pair key] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes: `clear` on a body is the DM's hunt (§5.5) — the quarry rule still binds (a `permitted` conclave is refused, `patrol_quarry_let_be`); the CROWN's order to sweep (THE BODIES §10.2 (b): "the crown may ORDER a sweep through PL's precedence … and PR's `PATROL_ORDER` by the crown") is this verb with the crown's own settlement, its riders reaching a member's body by the banner's right where that member believes the hold strong (PS's read, §10.3 (d)), on the ladder where it believes it weak] |
| `priority` | `first · avoid · clear · none` | `first` puts the place at the head of the round (a detachment if a threat is there) — on `round`, every rider the envelope allows rides (`fielded = max`); `avoid` keeps the riders off it (a DM who wants a road unwatched) — on `round`, the count holds at the minimum; `clear` sends a detachment at the place's den or band now; `none` clears the order [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — §3.2's "lift to `max` or hold at the minimum" now names its dial; a standing `first` on `round` held a season is §7.5's second trigger] |
| `wages` | `derived · unfunded · meagre · funded · lavish` | the PAY gate's CEILING (§3.1: the effective band is the lower of this and the purse's band — RULINGS-ROUND2b ST-r2-1) — the DM moves the input, never the count |
Host: the entry object lives in `patrols/patrolVerbs.js`; `realmManifest.js` (474) spreads it (+≤2), `realmVerbExecution.js` (663) dispatches to `patrols/patrolVerbApply.js` (+≤3) — the leaf-first idiom ST-0 measured. `realmVetoProse` reasons `patrol_gate_dark` · `patrol_place_unknown` · `patrol_none_fielded` · `patrol_quarry_let_be` (a `clear` on a body the court lets be — §5.5's quarry rule; FREE, `cure/D-PR-r4-probe/tokens-r4b.out`) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (5)]. never `{ kind: 'menace' }` (a menace is MN's `menace_met`) and never a site (a site is a WHERE and a subject, never an actor — A.7) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — SEAMS-r2-15] |
| `patrol_joins` | PR (PR-4) | the week a town asks an ally to join a detachment (§10.1(d)) | `rides · will_see · refused` | CLIMAX | `road (a, b)` — the shared stretch, else `country (ally)` | the ally's fork: the landed relationship pair state (label, trust, resentment) and its own believed danger (§10.1(d)) | the ally upon the asker |

**Read here, owned elsewhere (never a second card for one fact):** PS's four kinds (`passage_at_the_border` for a patrol crossing, and its ENVOY ARM for an envoy the riders stop — PR-r2-10; `armed_incursion` for an enemy in a third town's land, whose home-defence battle stays PS's while the riders' screen is PR's card — Q1); ST's `site_cleared` (a detachment clearing a den); MN's `menace_met` (a detachment grouped to a menace's band — MN's producer row; `army_beaten` reads "an army or a detachment", Q6); ES's `spy_at_target` at WHERE `road` (`caught · uncaught`, Q3); WY-6's `caravan_seized`; PT's `party_gate` at WHERE `road` and PT's road WATCH kind `party_ambush` (a `party_warrant` stands at the court's town — RULINGS-ROUND3 B, PT's row) (STOP-PR-2 RULED; PR-r2-13); PL's `call_in_answer` for the crown's call on a member's riders (PR-r2-17); THE BODIES' `bodies_meet` (two bodies met through this volume's ONE encounter law, §8.1 — BD's card), `body_stance` (the court's stance that decides whom the riders hunt, §5.5 — BD's card) and the hunt MISSION card of L-BD 40 (`hunt_taken`, its owning volume's — PR's detachment is only its unit) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes]. NEVER read here: `envoy_intercepted` · `envoy_held` — the army interception's; a patrol's hold waits on ES-2b (SEAMS-r2-2). **Not hearings:** the round, the cadence, grouping and dispersal, looks and their reports, the presence and counter leans, the witness read — receipts only (they decide nothing between two parties). [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final) — the brief's `patrol_engages` verdicts `repelled · withdrew · destroyed` and the screen's `screened · withdrew · destroyed`: `repelled` is ST's `site_cleared` verdict in the OPPOSITE sense (the clearer repelled) on the same docket, and `destroyed` is a landed literal in 27 files (institution and site events); ONE vocabulary covers both acts.]

**Requirement 24's three choices on every card:** LEAVE it (the default resolves at the next advance), TILT it (`slight · heavy`, a draw bias ±0.05 / ±0.15 on the contest — MN's idiom), or DECIDE it (the DM's word is the verdict; never "decree"). Only at a ONE-WEEK advance is a kind minted and paused; MONTH, SEASON and YEAR advances resolve every week on the default and leave each week's receipt; nothing queues. The proposals queue's anchor `targetSaveId` is always a real settlement (the riders' town, the ally); the counterparty rides the `subject` dial (RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH §4's actor convention). No PR kind is a party kind (PR19); requirement 24's party default never arises here. **The card** (the Evidence Law): "Kell's riders have met Merewick's van at the ford. Left alone, they hold it a week." · "Ashford asks Tolle's riders to ride with it against the band in the hills. Left alone, Tolle will see." The registry count rises by TWO kind names in two rows (the fold restates it at P4; `patrol_order` is withdrawn — PR-r2-17).

**The omniscient default's arm, named (RULINGS-AFTER-RESEARCH §7):** in `realistic_regional` (omniscient; no rumor road; the war layer dark) the riders still ride and their effects run where lit — reports are chronicle lines (the town knows the truth in its land), no false report exists, and no army is screened. **The host lighting-claim note:** "lit in a preset" is trusted only after the BIRTH-DOOR CHECK (CHAIR-NOTES 9: a born campaign carries the default preset's name but none of its switches). **The same-seed shift:** lighting `patrolEffectsEnabled` in any preset is a DECLARED same-seed shift (every embattled world's danger, banditry and smuggling move, and every war in which a court keeps riders marches a smaller army, which its opener judges feasible or not as the smaller army — some targets drop out of reach, PR-V1), signed at its lighting unit; lighting `patrolsEnabled` alone moves only PS's witness read (from always-witnessed to watched) — itself a declared shift where `passageEnabled` is lit.

**THE LIGHTING PRECONDITION** (a cross-program row; the map program's half): `patrolEffectsEnabled` lights in a world only where the map's patrol lens is ABSENT or has ADOPTED the host's patrol rows and its new legend (§13) — never where the lens still draws its own sway cadence under "patrols change nothing in the simulation" while the host's riders change danger; the lighting unit's certification row checks it, and the fold's map-side inbox carries the ask [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-14].
