## §13 THE WINDOW, NOT THE DOOR (the owner's decision in the host document; item 18) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — HOST-r2-5 (RULINGS-ROUND2b: "H16 is the map's INBOUND door, which the host never consumes; the host's patrol facts go OUT through the events WINDOW (H9) like every other fact") · HOST-r2-6 · PR-r2-14]

- **WHILE PR IS DARK** the map's patrols are its own picture — its sway-derived cadence (V13 §3.9 (14)), its legend true ("patrols change nothing in the simulation"); the host is asked for nothing (the owner's decision of 2026-10-18).
- **WHEN PR LIGHTS** the host SENDS its patrol facts OUT THROUGH THE WINDOW, as it sends every fact: the dated acts (a clash, a catch, a screen, a look's report) in the EVENTS window (H9: "committed facts only … each fact with who may see it"), and the week just lived per road half — `{ road: stretchKey, half: holder, power, command, cadence (PATROL_CADENCES), on (the week's PRESENCE as it was), state? (on_round · engaged · escorting), detachment? (band, aim), banner? (the power's stack's `rootId` — derived, so the map may draw the banner's colour; RULINGS-ROUND3 F.7) }` [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7 (F.7)], read from the placement digest's `lived` row (§2.7) — as a ledger row of the LENS window beside it (H23: "the ledger rows the host already keeps … each with who may see it"), where the map's patrol layer, which already takes its inputs from the polity window's patrol rows (H13: "the whole input of the sway and of patrols"), can draw it on M-W5's sway-and-patrols step (H25) [REFINED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — the chair named the events window (H9); the host document's own rows for a standing ledger fact are H23 beside H9, and H13 for the polity inputs — the row's name is the map program's]. Each fact is at most one advance stale and carries its resolved audience (DM-only by default; a covert power's riders fail closed, the map's own rule). THE HOST'S ASK (the map program decides; no FP text supersedes a map derivation — the fold's §3.1.26 ↳ r2b): draw PRESENCE AS SENT rather than recompute it from the cadence word — the map's own phase is keyed from the road segment's lineage, the host's from the road and the power, so two clocks would part and a drawn rider would stand beside "Kell's company slipped through unseen" (HOST-r2-6) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the FOLD-r2-verify minor ("A rule on the map program"; INBOX (h) frames it as an ask): PR, the wording's source, re-words "the map DRAWS PRESENCE AS SENT and never recomputes it" as the host's ask]. The cadence word means the same on both sides (a period, PR4) and DWELLS (§2.3), so a drawn picture changes with the world, not with each week's re-bucketing. The host asks, too, that the lens's legend change when it draws the host's rows ("the host's riders"; its regime banner states it — the map's honesty rule). A CROSS-PROGRAM NOTE back to the map program, for the fold's §3.1 map-side inbox (h): "draw the host's patrol rows when sent; your legend changes then" (the chair's row at P4).
- **H16 STAYS SHUT, AND OPEN AS THE OWNER LEFT IT.** H16 is the map's INBOUND door ("patrol coverage re-priced through one distance reader, read only by outcome rolls"; "The only door through which patrols or the sway can ever touch the simulation") — the door through which the MAP's coverage might one day touch the host. PR never opens it and never consumes the map's coverage: the host's riders derive from the host's own garrison, wages, security and belief (§3); the map's `patrolOffer` stays an offer no host reader reads (the host document: "No host consumer exists today, and none is asked for"). PR therefore does NOT answer trade-OQ4 ("a host meaning for patrol coverage" — the MAP's coverage, which the owner decided not to ask) and claims nothing on trade-OQ6; `route_wardenship` stays EC-6's stance term (OD-FC-2), a commercial reason between two courts, not a patrol.
- **CADENCE NEVER REACHES THE HOST AS A TIME** from the map; the host sends its own cadence as a FACT (a word) with its own presence, and the map's law ("the host prices any round trip with its own distance reader") is untouched because the host's riders are priced by the host's own reader (`hopWeeks`). Water carries no patrol fact (PR15).
- **THE LIGHTING PRECONDITION** (§12): `patrolEffectsEnabled` lights only where the map's lens is absent or has adopted these rows and the new legend.

## §14 THE WAVES (every wave: car-0 · leaves · hosts · FIRST-VISIBLE · TIER · DEGRADED ARM · EDITOR (a forks · b seals and predicates · c the counterparty · d the tick and states · e the record) · Alignment · `causedBy`)

PR is a coupling CARRIER across **WAR · TRADE · INFO · POPULATIONS**; its prefix `PR` is chartered with its first coupling-registry row (requirement 21(a)/(c)). HBF rows minted, each in its wave's commit (L10(a)): `patrolEngagesContest`, `patrolNotice`, `patrolLook`. Chain **C16 THE PATROLS** sits AFTER C15 THE SITES, ADJACENT — C14 · C15 · C16 share the border gate and the fort (RULINGS-ROUND2b open question 7, RULED); after C0 · C0.5 · C1 — and, by CHAIR-NOTES 18 (c) (vetoable; the owner's 21:52 "i leave to your recommendation"), BEHIND the map's M-W2 and the war layer's lighting ("MOVE C14 THE PASSAGE and C16 THE PATROLS behind the map's M-W2 and the war layer's lighting (… they do nothing in the default preset)"), C14 still beside it; the fold's §6 states the order at P4 [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X4: this placement predated 18 (c)]; it reads both, and PS's `witnessedCrossing` waits on it; its FIRST FELT CHAIN is PR-0 → PR-1 (riders on the roads; the slow and fast halves). Its map prerequisites, for the fold's §6 (HOST-r2-12's row): H13 / H23 on M-W5's sway-and-patrols step plus H25 for the window rows — never H16 [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — Q7 · HOST-r2-5].

**PR-0 — THE RIDERS AND THE ROUND (`patrolsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0 (MEASUREMENTS before any PR line):** (i) the stage host — `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` (461) with MN's and ST's leaves beside PR's (+3); MN-0's four STOP conditions inherited; ⛔ STOP at build if no room — PR's stage follows MN-0/ST-0's host; (ii) the census of every PR token on the then-current master (`patrol`, `on_round`, `driven_off`, `cut_down`, `will_see`, the cadence words); (iii) the census population's distribution WITHIN each tier across the soak corpus (the pool ratio's real spread; `cure/D-PR-r2-probe/pool-r2.out` is the arithmetic, the corpus the measure) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-2]; (iv) the `MOVEMENT_SITES` row for `patrols/patrolMove.js`; (v) the placement digest's size on the soak corpus (rows × halves; the drop-when-empty pin).
- **Leaves:** `patrols/patrolLedger.js` (the one writer; the four maps' normalizer; drop-when-empty; the `losses` recovery and horizon; FNV ids), `patrols/envelope.js` (`patrolEnvelopeOf`, §3), `patrols/round.js` (`roundOf`, `onStretchThisWeek`, `watchOn`, `watchOf`, `coverageOf`, `patrolPlacementOf`), `patrols/patrolMove.js` (the leg price — `namedPersonLegTicks`), `patrols/patrolStage.js :: advancePatrols` (§4.1 steps 1, 2, 5), `patrols/witness.js` (`witnessedCrossing`). **Host edits:** `calamityKernel.js` (+3); PS's `passageBorderLeaf.js` imports `witnessedCrossing` instead of its `true` stub (PS's commit or PR's, whichever lands second).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the dossier's defence page the advance the flag lights — "Kell keeps riders on the north road and the ford road every week" (surface: the dossier; the map's lens behind its patrol toggle once it draws the window's rows, §13); scrutiny: the four gates in words ("a town; its people enough for four patrols; its purse meagre; its watch firm").
- **TIER:** every preset; premium; aspatial-safe (graph hops); the omniscient arm identical (no belief involved in the round) · **DEGRADED ARM:** no canon ⇒ stretches are graph edges and the danger arm (PR-1) is dark; no `hearingWeekEnabled` ⇒ irrelevant here (no hearing in PR-0).
- **EDITOR:** (a) forks — none (a roving half's slot and its rover's base are `fnv1a32` keys of the half and the power, §2.3, registered in HBF as `patrolPhase` with no draw) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the carry · PR-V9]; (b) predicates `patrolsActive`, `onStretchThisWeek`; (c) the counterparty — none; (d) the tick — weekly; the placement written once for the next tick and read by every consumer (PR4); no institution or person write; (e) the record — `spatialLedgers.patrols` (§2.7) is worldState-side lived history outside the record register and `rederive`; `PATROL_COMMANDS · PATROL_STATES · PATROL_CADENCES · DETACHMENT_AIMS` are save-data contract.
- **Alignment (requirement 13):** none at PR-0 — the envelope reads tier, people, purse and order; alignment enters at PR-1 (§7.5) and PR-2 (the court's forks).
- **`causedBy`:** the envelope's receipt names its four gates' reads; a stretch's cadence names the round's priority reasons.

**PR-1 — THE EFFECTS (`patrolEffectsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** measure `banditryLoss`'s and `smuggleSuccessChance`'s callers (exactly `commodityFlow.js:651`, `supplyShipments.js:372`, `commodityFlow.js:602` at master — any new caller gets `fireLevel` / `watch01` or is listed as reading none); measure ST's one additive slot leaf (`siteTermAt`) for a second row (`criminal_corridor`) — ⛔ STOP at build if ST-1 has not landed AND `stressorDynamics.js` cannot take ≤ 2 lines (then the counter term waits on ST-1; it NEVER rides `menaceCounterTerms` — MN's slot (2) is filled through the stamp, and MN's own fallback reads `siteTermAt` directly) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-7]; the deploy-draw host — `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` (685), +2, the drawn capacity right after `fromCap` and BEFORE both feasibility gates and the target loop, and the stamp's ONE conditional line beside the seed (`seedDeploymentState`, `warArmyRecord.js` 72 eff, is not edited: the seed comes after the gates, and the stamp rides the record the way `joinLedger` and `casusReasons` do) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-1] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V1] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W2]; measure whether THE SHARED PRESENCE COMPOSER stands — if not, MN's OWNED unit is cut with PR-1 (its manifest row and its text MN's, CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — MN-V6] [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X4]; measure `commonsGrievance01`'s input registration path (§7.5's PLAUSIBLE); measure the correlation of `buildStrengthLookup`'s index with `offensive` (the prefilter's `drawShare01`, §6.6, PLAUSIBLE); measure the four-week ring and the joint floor (§6.3) against the soak corpus.
- **Leaves:** `patrols/patrolEffects.js` (`patrolSubtraction`, `patrolCounterTerm`, `patrolDrawOf`, `patrolDrawnCap`, the `BRIBE_KEEP` table); `patrols/patrolDrawStamp.js` (`normalizePatrolDrawStamp`); and — ONLY if C16 is the first of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 to be cut — MN's OWNED composer unit cut with it, `spatial/presenceComposer.js :: presenceTerms` (its neutral home, MN §3.3; its text and its manifest row MN's, CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d); never a PR re-implementation) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X4]. PR's subtraction is ONE row of that module's closed registration list, registered if the car stands. **Host edits:** `embattlement.js` (+2: `banditryLoss`'s `fireLevel`; +≤2 more ONLY where MN's composer unit is cut with PR-1 — the composer's call before `stepEmbattlement`, MN §4.2's seam) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — MN-V6] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W5: the feeder row lived in `embattlement.js` and the composer's module went unnamed]; `commodityFlow.js` (+2: `fireLevel` and the gate town's `watch01`); `supplyShipments.js` (+1); `smuggle.js` (+2); `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` (685, +2: the drawn capacity right after `fromCap`, read by both feasibility gates, the prefilter and the seed; the stamp's conditional line beside the seed, §6.6) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V1] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — PR-W2]; `worldState.js :: normalizeDeployments` (351, +1: the stamp's fail-closed block through `patrols/patrolDrawStamp.js :: normalizePatrolDrawStamp`, §16.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-V7]; ST's `siteEffects.js` (+1 row: the corridor; the raider row is ST's slot); `commonsVoiceKernel.js` (+≤2, PLAUSIBLE).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the week riders stand on an embattled stretch a caravan crosses — the trade receipt "The caravan reached Kell untouched; Kell's riders were on the hill road" (scrutiny: the fast half's reason) · the season the slow half takes a country raided by dens or roaming brigands below the latch's exit (never while a menace raids it, §6.1) — the dossier's threat page "Kell's riders have the roads again" [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — SEAMS-r2-7 (b), PR's echo] · a smuggling catch — "Kell's riders confiscated Varn cloth run past the embargo".
- **TIER:** the danger arm (§6.1) canonized only (the embattlement marker); the counter term in every stressor preset; the fast and smuggling halves under `commodityFlowEnabled` (the commerce physics); the draw under `warLayerEnabled`; premium · **DEGRADED ARM:** aspatial — the counter term and the draw only.
- **EDITOR:** (a) forks — none new (the leans ride the hosts' own keyed draws); (b) the predicates `watched`, `patrolEffectsActive`; (c) real settlements only (`watchOn` / `watchOf` name powers that exist); (d) the hosts' ticks; (e) the deployment record's conditional `patrolDraw` stamp (§2.7); every lean otherwise derived from the placement.
- **Alignment:** §7.5's harassment input reads the seat's `settlementAlignment` (a lawful-cruel seat keeps riders out and pays the grievance gladly).
- **`causedBy`:** each lean's receipt names the stretch, the power and the week (the slow half the four-week window).

**PR-2 — REACT, GROUP, DISPERSE; LOOKS; REPORTS; THE CLASH (`patrolsEnabled`).**

## §15 PATROLS CONSIDERED — the coverage table and the token register

### 15.1 THE COVERAGE TABLE — one row per FP program (each existing volume carries ONE "patrols considered" line citing its row, RULINGS-ROUND2 E)
| Program | How it considers patrols |
|---|---|
| SP (the spine) | SP-9's docket hosts PR's two kinds (`registerEngagementKind`); the actor convention unchanged (riders are a settlement's) |
| CW (C0.5 THE CAUSE WALK) | every PR receipt carries `causedBy`; the walk reaches a catch, a screen, a look and a lean with no wiring |
| GR (grammar) | no PR term; a peace's `site_cession` or a treaty moves the round through the sites and the ladder by construction |
| IN (information) | reports are rumor feed entries at hop 0 (§9.3); IN's plant road makes false reports (§7.4); a road can be made to LOOK watched or empty (§8.3) |
| ES (espionage) | the patrolled road is ES's THIRD exposure class — ONE roll, PR's notice, with ES's competence once in ES's own form and ES's outcome (`spy_at_target`, WHERE `road`) (§8.2); J-ES-1's amendment bracket is the fold lane's (the chair's ruling, Q3); ES's product row is a `believedWatch` producer, and mission planning reads it |
| TR / EC (trade, economy) | the fast half at `banditryLoss` (per hop, the roll only); the gate town's own riders at `smuggle.js`'s worst gate; EC-6's stance makes goods contraband; EC-18's open road and the caravans time departures by `believedWatch`; `route_wardenship` stays EC-6's stance term (OD-FC-2), never a patrol |
| WF (faith) | none of its own — a creed's people act through the town's own numbers; a creed's moving flock is THE BODIES' conclave, whom the riders let be, watch or hunt by the court's stance (§5.5; BD §7.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes] |
| POP (populations) | columns escorted away from a front (§10.4); road deaths read the danger ledger (by construction); the harassment input to the commons' ONE grievance home (§7.5) |
| INT (the interior) | NOT APPLICABLE — factions read the town's own numbers; the will gate reads the town's order (`defenseLedger.internal`), never a faction |
| WY (wayfare) | caravans' `caravan_seized` odds fall with an escort; the host's patrol rows go out through the window (§13), never through H16; a patrol's map piece is a presence, never an intention |
| HB (habit) | the round's habit term (§2.2): HB's ONE curve on the settlement actor under the landed class `route_menaced`, the stretch key its action token — HB's car-0 admits the token or the term stays dark (no thirteenth class); the round's cadence is a habit a body learns through belief (§2.3), and a hunt's leader's axes and a body's own habits are HB's person and body grains (§5.5) — PR mints no learning law [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — L-BD 39] |
| WC (war circulation) | the kept riders leave the army's SEED at deploy (the draw, §6.6) — never the per-tick capacity; a corps (CH's unit mark) crosses as a military unit (§8.2); WC-12's located brigands ARE THE BODIES' gangs, met on the road in `patrol_engages` by PR's band contest — WC-12's `EMBATTLED_RESOLUTIONS` close a brigand SIEGE, never a road fight (§5.3) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's route (6)]; WC-1 credits allied joins; WC-5's call-ins go out in the screen's week; WC's people ledger is the slot for riders' headcounts |
| EP (the advance epoch) | long advances run the round and every hearing on defaults; nothing queues |
| PL (the polity) | THE BANNER (§10.3): a patrol's loyalty stack is PL's `polityOf(…).path`, derived; the crown's share of a member's riders is PL's one `crownReachAt` share (F.2, PL's row); the NET's call reads the crown's OWN hold (`polityHoldOf(…, rootId, rootId)`, PL §3.1 (e)) while a crossing's right is PS's read of the HOLDER's belief (PS §5.5); PL's `swornLinkSuspended`, PL §6's feuds and a break break the rung; the call reaches riders rung by rung through PL's summons under every hold below `unbroken` and DIRECTLY under `unbroken`, never skipping a lord's ROW (the mid-lord's credit or precedence friction, PL §4.1 (ii); §10.3 (b) — STOP-PR-r4-1 as RE-RULED); a member lends `max(1, ⌊n × s × hold01⌋)` above the home's minimum, a sub-vassal through its liege's line (F.2, B.8); PL's PROTECTION rows read `bannerCoverOf`'s SEASON TALLY (F.4, B.9) and its SERVICE row credits lent riders (F.6) — PL's writer, PR requests no row; the swept lord's grudge is PL's `riders_quartered` ROW with its one resentment patch (§7.6; F.6 (b), A.8) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — STOP-PR-r4-1 · B.8 · B.9 · A.8] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7]; the crown's call on a member's riders is PL-5's `call_in_answer` (`half_answered` keeps them home) — no PR kind; feuds turn riders hostile (§10.1(f)); the crown's embargo makes goods contraband (`confiscation`); the `patrol:` episode prefix PL registers prices a PATROL's passage (PS's request, PL-V4), never a PR fealty row; PL §15.12's "the CROWN'S ROAD WARDENS" reads "the crown's riders" in PL's own disposition (SEAMS-r2-17) |
| MN (the menace) | MN §5.5's slot (2) is filled THROUGH THE STAMP (PR's term enters ST's slot once); riders subtract inside MN §3.3's ONE composition — THE SHARED PRESENCE COMPOSER, MN's OWNED unit, cut with whichever chain is cut first (A.3; CHAIR-NOTES 18 (d)) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PR-X4] — on the suppressible part only; a raiding menace keeps an embattled town embattled, its level easing (MN M53); a grouped detachment meets a menace through MN's `menace_met` (a producer row; `army_beaten` reads "an army or a detachment"); riders report a menace (the court picture) |
| RN (renown) | catches, clashes, clearings and screens are candidate engine-source deed rows (`patrol_catch`, `patrol_clash`) — ONE ACT, ONE DEED (B.5): an unled clash the riders' town's TALE, a patrol cut down by a named body the BODY's deed (its RN key `g:<bodyId>`, its creed's for a conclave; its leading paragon `ledBy`), a LED hunt's success CH's `hunt_won` at the mission's close and never a `patrol_clash` [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — B.5 · the RN→PR route] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4)]; a paragon's fame AT THE PATROL'S TOWN (`fameIn`) cuts its cover (§8.1); the join's default reads the landed pair state, never RN's court regard (RN's refused pairs) |
| CH (champions) | a warden leads riders through `paragonLean(…, 'wildland', 'patrol_rider_quality')` (A.10), a magistrate raises the will (§10.5); a warden HOLDING the public-security office knows the hidden ways of its land (L-BD 41, §5.5; never a warden merely retained, B.13); a paragon on a known way is met only by the trail arm (§8.1); a paragon may LEAD a hunt by CH's purpose `hunt_body` [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — CH→PR routes 1–3 · B.13], moving by its own mover on PR's one prediction (`predictedPlaceOf`), and its failure is CH's `paragon_disgraced` (L-BD 40) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4)]; a hostile paragon is turned back by CH's gate words; a villain's runners carry covert cover and buy unpaid riders; a report feeds the call board |
| PT (the party) | the party meets riders on the road only through PT's `party_gate` at WHERE `road` (STOP-PR-2 RULED); PT's WATCH on the road (`party_ambush`; a `party_warrant` stands at the court's town) is a detachment of the court's riders (PR19); PT consumes no `believedWatch` (the DM sees all); a look may find the party's traces (PT §11's visibility); a body never acts toward the party (BD15) and a party meeting a body is PT's road arm, never a PR card |
| PS (the passage) | PR OWNS `witnessedCrossing` (the holder's riders on its half of the gate's road); PS's escort is a detachment; a patrol crossing a border is PS's mover (`PASSAGE_MOVER_KINDS` holds `patrol` and `corps`) and an envoy the riders stop is heard on PS's envoy arm (§6.5); the screen is the third registrant in PS §4.1's ONE order and is held by PS's ONE helper in its FREEZE mode, inside PS9's one cap where PS is lit (A.2); PS's unit key (the deployment id) holds across a screened week; PS's enemy-column feeder reaches the destination's own land (Q2); THE BANNER: PS's jurisdiction read answers same-kingdom riders BY RIGHT where the HOLDER believes the crown's hold strong (a `banner` permit, no spend, one card defaulting `granted` — PS §5.5, OD-PS-45), on the ally rung with the `quiet` spend where it believes it weak, and by the believed label across a broken rung — the crown's riders a foreign unit in a rebel's land (§10.3 (d), (g); PS's half); a BODY is never a passage mover — its crossing is PR's encounter (PS1, BD's route to PS); an ENEMY's patrol on a foreign stretch opens PS's `armed_incursion` with NO PS border battle (no transit record), riders meeting riders PR's `patrol_engages` (PS §5.5, minor (g)); a patrol never CROSSES a border by a hidden way (PS §5.5; PR §5.5's franchise rides ways only inside its own land) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — PS's round-5 route · B.4] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7] |
| ST (the sites) | riders subtract inside the one suppressor slot and cap; a fort puts its halves first on its holder's round, and the keep's GARRISON is the site's own complement — its road rows stand lit or dark, a distinct feeder of the same capped suppressor (§10.5; RULINGS-ROUND3 A.1) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — PR-r2-8 · PR-V2]; a watchtower extends notice; a grouped detachment opens `site_cleared`; the PAY band is ST's one band law, exported settlement-keyed (§3.1) |
| BD (THE BODIES, `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_BD.md`) | a body is a traveller of the ONE encounter law (its own stage calls it; MET on a trail only where the power knows the trail — `trailWatchOf`, zero otherwise) and its second WATCHER when two bodies meet (BD's `bodies_meet`) (§8.1); the riders pursue only a `hunted` gang, an `outlawed` conclave or a beast, watch a `curbed` conclave and let the rest be (§5.5); THE HUNT predicts on `believedBodies`, the known trails and `BODY_HABITS`, goes cold, leaves the round unridden and shadows when outmatched (§5.5); a body's road fight is `patrol_engages` with BD's `bodyStrength` (§11.2); a known trail's junction is a round stop (§2.2); a body planning a route reads `believedWatch`, banner-blind (§8.3); WARTIME IS THE GANGS' SEASON (§9.1 (e)); PR's subtraction and BD's presence row sit in the one composer (§6.1); and from round 5 — the known set read BY NAME (`knownTrailsOf`, B.13), the ways on master's hidden rung through `hiddenWayEdges` (B.4), the prediction on `believedBodyOf` (its informer rows), BD's `ROAD_COVER_BY_MIGHT` and the commons' +0.2 on the encounter's cover, the riders' gleaning roll and the `watched` mark PR's stage writes through BD's one writer, the gang's `bribe` on PR's unpaid-wages slot, a LED hunt's card BD's (`hunt_taken`, lit with `bodyEffectsEnabled`) [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — BD's routes (8)–(11) · BD's round-5 routes (a)–(g)] [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — BD's routes (1)–(7)] |
| LG (logistics) | journeys and fleets read the danger ledger (by construction); the sea's watch is the port's navy (W-NAVY, PR15) |

| create | only `patrolLedger.js` from the stage (§4.1 step 4: a believed threat, a hunt, a look, an escort, a join's `rides`, the DM's `clear`) | only `patrolLedger.js` at a `cut_down` verdict (or default) | only `patrolVerbApply.js` (an approved `PATROL_ORDER`) |
| read | the stage (advance, disperse, rest), the hearings' resolvers, `watchOn` (an engaged or escorting detachment covers its stretch), the dossier, the map window (§13) | the envelope (§3.1: the count minus unrecovered losses) | the envelope (`wages`), the round (`place`, `priority`) |

### 16.2 THE SCENARIO MATRIX (each row a pin family at its wave; a MEASURABLE assertion, or DARK)
| # | Scenario | Assertion | Wave |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | ALL FLAGS DARK | the hashed dormancy golden over the soak corpus is byte-identical; no `patrols` key on any world; PS's `witnessedCrossing` answers `true` | PR-0 |
| R2 | THE ENVELOPE BY TIER | a hamlet fields ≤ 1 patrol of band ≤ `thin`; a thorp 0; a metropolis ≤ 12 with no single patrol above `capable`; for every settlement `fielded ≤ max ≤ ceiling` whatever the wages; the count is never read from a stored field; WITHIN A TIER a smaller census fields fewer — a 901-soul town's pool 1, a 2,951-soul town's 4; a 25,001-soul metropolis's 5 (`cure/D-PR-r2-probe/pool-r2.out`) — the within-tier population pin [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-2] | PR-0 |
| R3 | PEACETIME — Kell, a funded town, nothing believed near | `fielded = ceil(max × peaceShare)` (0.6 where the posture layer is dark); the rider-time allocation (§2.3) gives the standing riders their halves `weekly` and the rovers their blocks at a fixed slot — each rider on ONE place a week, an `every_four_weeks` half present exactly one week in four (R31) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — the carry]; two runs agree on every half-week (PR4); no hearing | PR-0 |
| R4 | THE UNPAID GARRISON — Kell drops to `Struggling` (`unfunded`) | `fielded` falls by `PAY_SHARE` 0.25; the band steps down one; `BRIBE_KEEP` 0.4 halves the smuggling watch; the counter term turns negative against the tier's reference; a fort Kell holds is abandoned by want after a season (ST §6.1 step 3) | PR-0/PR-1 |
| R5 | THE SLOW HALF — a raiding menace holds the hill road; Kell's riders walk it `weekly` for a season | the presence term at Kell falls by `0.15 × coverage` within ST's 0.25 shared cap; the walls term never falls; Kell exits the latch only when the term stays below `EXIT_THRESHOLD` (0.3) for `MIN_DWELL_TICKS` (6) — so while the menace raids, an embattled Kell STAYS embattled and its level eases toward the suppressed raid part (MN M53) [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — SEAMS-r2-7 (b)] | PR-1 |

## §18 WHAT A DM READS (deeds only, never forecasts; no numerals; the surface shows the outcome, the WHY waits on scrutiny)

1. "Kell keeps riders on the north road and the ford road every week." *(the dossier's defence page; scrutiny: the four gates — "a town; people enough for four patrols; its purse meagre; its watch firm")*
2. "The hill road is dangerous, but Kell's riders are on it this week." *(the fast half — the trade receipt or the believed-watch card a merchant reads)*
3. "The caravan reached Kell untouched; Kell's riders were on the hill road." *(a goods receipt)*
4. "Kell's riders confiscated Varn cloth run past the embargo." *(surface; scrutiny: the stance, Kell's gate, the riders' pay — an embargo gate's landed branch is `confiscation`, a hostile gate's `seizure`)* [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r2) — PR-r2-23]
5. "Kell's riders went up to the hills and found nothing but old fires." / "Kell's riders report a den at the old fort." *(a look; the second carries the zero-click suggestion "— mark it?")*
6. "Kell's riders rode out together against the band at the old fort." *(a detachment; the clearing card follows)*
7. "Kell's riders came in from the north road with word of Merewick's host at the ford." *(the war's first sign in Kell — pushed as the war's cause)*
8. "Kell's riders fell at the ford; the levy had its week." *(the screen, `cut_down`)*
9. "With the watch gone to the war, the hill road has gone to the brigands." — and where Kell holds a keep on that road: "With the watch gone to the war, the hill road beyond the keep's reach has gone to the brigands." *(the riders Kell kept rode the army's road; the hill road was beyond them; a held keep's own garrison still holds its stretches, so the sentence names only the road it no longer holds — scrutiny: the kept count, the counter term's fall, the keep's rows, the dens' rise)* [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r3) — A.1: "PR's felt sentence 9 is restated under the Evidence Law with the keep as the exception"]
10. "Ashford's riders took a Kell spy on the river road." · "Ashford's riders turned back Kell's envoy on the hill road." *(catches, by their owners' words)*
11. "Tolle's riders rode with Ashford's against the band in the hills." *(`patrol_joins` → `rides`)*
12. "The crown's riders keep the Tolle hills since Kell's riders went to the war." · "No rider of the crown has come to the Tolle hills since Kell's riders went to the war." *(THE BANNER's net covering — or failing — a member whose riders went to the levy: RULINGS-ROUND3 F.7, PL §4.7 F.7's two lines; scrutiny: the stack as the chain, the hold, the need; the deed PL records)* [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7: the r3 sentence "The crown of Merewick's riders keep Kell's roads" named the crown's presence, not the protection the owner asked to express]
13. "Kell's envoy took the hill road; Kell did not know Ashford's riders were on it." *(planning on belief; scrutiny: what Kell believed)*
14. *(an unwatched stretch writes NOTHING a DM reads about the holder; on scrutiny of the round the DM sees "the east road is watched each season")*
15. "Merewick's riders were turned back at the Kell ford, where they rode freely last season." *(the first sign of a broken rung — F.5; scrutiny: the break, the believed label)*
16. "Kell's riders caught the Tolle gang on the drovers' path; the watch knows the way now." · "Kell's riders lost the Tolle gang in the forest and came home." *(THE HUNT — a catch on a way; a cold trail; scrutiny: the prediction's three reasons)* [REFINED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — "lost the Tolle gang's trail" re-worded: `trail` is the map's RUNG word on the same surface (SEAMS-r4-16), so the DM line says what happened without it]
17. "Kell's warden knows the drover's way; the hill gang has moved its den across the border." *(the warden's ways — deterrence by belief; scrutiny: the office, the known set)*
18. "Captain Aldric led Kell's riders after the Hollow Road gang and drove it from the hills." *(a hunt led — L-BD 40; the leader named, never harmed by the engine)*
19. "The Hollow Road gang has learned Kell's riders come on the fourth week." *(scrutiny only — the learnable clock, L-BD 39; never pushed)* [CURED 2026-10-18 (fp-final r4) — A.7 · BD's routes · L-BD 39 · 40 · 41: five sentences for the new deeds, each who · which power · what effect]
20. "Kell's riders followed the Tolle gang as far as the ford; the watch knows the way that far now." *(GLEANING IN PURSUIT — L-BD 11e; scrutiny: the snow, the gang's haste, the warden leading)* [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — L-BD 11e]
21. "Tolle took it ill that the crown called Ashford's riders past it." *(THE RIDE PAST — PL's precedence friction under `unbroken`, §10.3 (b); scrutiny: the summons, Tolle's answer, the row)* [CURED 2026-10-19 (fp-final r5) — STOP-PR-r4-1 as RE-RULED]

## §19 OPEN QUESTIONS FOR THE CHAIR (round 5a first; each also in `cure/D-PR-r5.md` §6; the round-4a and round-3a lists kept below as history)

ROUND 5a (none blocking; each vetoable):
1. **The riders' hidden franchise** (OD-PR-51, §5.5): B.4 re-points PR's seams to master's rung, whose access law "fails CLOSED"; PR declares ONE token, `patrol`, passed only for a `pursue` detachment within capacity on a way its town knows (the owner's 18:33 warden word, 22:19 gleaning word), the form B.4 gave THE BODIES' `body`. The alternative: no patrol rides a way at all (a warden-led hunt waits at the junctions; the warden's word then reaches knowledge only) — PS §5.5's "it never takes a hidden way" would then read as written.
2. **The sweep counts a threat need too** (§7.6): PL's F.6 (b) (i) reads the ONE season tally, so the banner's riders on a lord's halves in a season with no cover occasion are friction whatever placed them — the crown's help with a threat the lord's own riders could not reach included (a lord that wants help without the grudge asks, `patrol_joins`). The alternative needs a second field (help weeks apart from sweep weeks), beyond B.9's one-field addition.
3. **The tally counts the whole season** (§2.7, §10.3 (f)): a net rider placed on another need in the weeks BEFORE a member's men went to the levy counts toward that season's `protection_kept`; PL's late-answer clause covers only an occasion opened in the season's last month. The alternative: the pair also carries the first covered tick and PL counts only weeks after the answer (a second sub-field).
4. **The root's home minimum** (REFINED, §10.3 (c)): the crown keeps `NET_HOME_MIN` of its own riders on its round like any member; the alternative lets `hold01` 1.0 lend them all.
5. **The hunt among a body's friends** (REFINED, §7.5): BD §7.6 says a hunt where the commons support the body costs the court the commons' grievance through PR's `W_HARASS`; PR carries it as a second arm of its ONE input (the greater of the two, never their sum). The alternative: BD strikes the clause and such a hunt costs nothing.
6. PL's own question (`cure/D-PL-r5.md` §7, not PR's to answer): F.6 (c)'s peacetime lend is priced only as `riders_lent` WEAR, and the weave is lit nowhere; with B.8 the lend now fires. PR's composition is unchanged by either answer.

ROUND 4a (history; Q1 RULED in RULINGS-ROUND4 §E and APPLIED in round 5a — §10.3 (b), R42):

1. **⛔ STOP-PR-r4-1 — the call rung by rung at `unbroken`** (§10.3 (b), §17): F.1's "never skipping a lord" against PL §4.1's `unbroken` direct summons; the proposed re-ruling routes the direct summons THROUGH each mid-lord's `call_in_answer` in the same week; the alternative gives F.1 an `unbroken` exception.
2. **`drawShare01` over the points ACTUALLY drawn** (§6.6, PR-W3): the chair ruled "guards 0/0 as 0"; PR keeps that guard and takes the share only over a draw that happened, so a court with no offensive to draw from reads 1 (the landed prefilter) — EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r4-probe/drawshare.out`: the hard gate admits a zero-offensive attacker against a collapsing defender (`require_betrayal`), which a literal 0 would skip although no rider was drawn. The literal alternative: `drawShare01 = fromCap.offensive > 0 ? … : 0` with `points` uncapped (a stamp may then claim more points than the army had).
3. (Withdrawn in this round — aligned to PL §4.7 F.2: the crown's own riders join the net at `share01` 1 × `hold01`, the crown's own read.)
4. **The net lends only on a NEED** (§10.3 (d): a levy-emptied member, a threat its riders cannot reach, the crown's war roads and trade, the crown's own peace call) — so a quiet strong kingdom's members keep their own rounds; the alternative composes every member's lent share over every unwatched shared half every week, which makes the owner's "operate in unity" constant and the peace call's resentment ever-present.
5. **A net rider witnesses for the member whose half it stands on** (PR10 REFINED) — the GATE's catch stays the holder's (J385); the alternative keeps the witness the holder's own riders' only.
6. **A non-paragon leader is a reference, never moved** (§5.5): its residency stays home while its name leads the riders (macro over micro); the alternative moves the office-holder's ledger record with the detachment (a second mover on a person the W-H3 loop already moves).
7. **L-BD 40's "`suppressed`/`outlawed`"** for a hunted conclave (read under §G.5 as `curbed`/`outlawed`) against BD §7.1's "`curbed` is watched, not hunted": PR follows BD §7.1 (a mission never hunts a `curbed` flock) and routes the reconciliation to BD.

ROUND 3a (history):

The eight round-1 questions are RULED (RULINGS-ROUND2b) and applied: (1) PR hosts the screen card, PS keeps the home-defence battle (§9.1(b)); (2) PS's enemy-column feeder is widened to the destination's own land (§9.1(c)); (3) the road catch is `spy_at_target` at WHERE `road`, J-ES-1's bracket the fold lane's (§8.2); (4) PS9's one cap covers screens (§9.1(b)); (5) the fold brackets the §5 `patrol` row, OD-FC-2 ↳ and the §3.1 WC inbox (3) as superseded — the fold lane's act, which PR's §0.2 already marks; (6) MN's `army_beaten` reads "an army or a detachment" — MN's bracket (§5.3); (7) C16 sits after C15, adjacent (§14); (8) STOP-PR-0 is accepted, STOP-PR-1 and STOP-PR-2 are ruled, OD-PR-32 is kept (§17).

THE ROUND-2b QUESTIONS ARE RULED (RULINGS-ROUND3): (1) one body of men → A.1, TWO bodies of men under one cap — the keep's garrison is the site's own complement, its rows stand lit or dark (OD-PR-41 re-written; `fortWill` struck); (2) SEAMS-r2-7 → MN's sentence stands, and PR's echo of "falls out of the latch" is restated by the landed exit law (§6.1); (3) the window's row → H9 for dated acts, H23 for the week just lived, H13 the map's patrol inputs — the row's name stays the map program's, and PR's "the map never recomputes" is re-worded as the host's ASK (§13).

STILL OPEN (none blocking; each vetoable):
1. **The rider-time allocation** (OD-PR-42, §2.3): the first half always stands, so a one-rider town walks one road only (36 of 45 halves `never` at `n = 1`, `H` 1..9); the alternative — every half every four weeks, the first half included — spreads one rider thinner and drops "the army's road first". The chair may prefer it; R31 holds under both.
2. **The harassment's excess gate** (§7.5, REFINED): the ruling's trigger is the fork; PR adds that the fork must ACTUALLY add riders past the median share, so two towns fielding the same riders never differ in grievance (`harass.out`: 6 of the fork's 36 rows). The chair may strike the gate (then the bare fork grieves a wary one- or two-rider town).
3. **The prefilter's draw share** (§6.6): `CONQUEST_MARGIN` compares `buildStrengthLookup`'s `settlementStrength` index, not `offensive`; PR scales the attacker's index by `drawShare01` — PLAUSIBLE that the index tracks `offensive`, measured at PR-1's car-0.
